iraṇṭām pākam : itu oru stāyiyil vaḻaṅkum
curam curutikaḷaippaṟṟip palar collum apippirāyaṅ
kaḷai ottuppārttu avaikaḷ taṟkāla aṉupōkattiṟku
ottu varātavaiyeṉṟu teḷivuṟak kāṭṭum.



















karuṇāmirta cākaram
mutal puttakam - iraṇṭām pākam
poruḷ aṭṭavaṇai
   
kaṭavuḷ tuṇai.

karuṇāmirta cākaram.
mutal puttakam.
iraṇṭām pākam.
irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷ.
ciṟappuṟṟōṅkiya nam intiyāvil curutikaḷaip paṟṟic collum ovvoruvarum cāraṅkatēvar colliya tuvāvimcati curutikaḷ 22 ivaitāṉ eṉṟum, ivaikaḷāyirukkilāmeṉṟum, ivaikaḷāyirukku meṉṟu nām eṇṇalāmeṉṟum, nām niccayikkalāmeṉṟum colva tiṉālum, teṉṉintiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷil cilarum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ 22 eṉṟum, cāraṅkaruṭaiya apip pirāyam itutāṉ eṉṟum colvatiṉālum, mutal mutal tuvāvimcati curutikaḷaip paṟṟi maṟṟavar collum apippirāyaṅkaḷaiyum cāraṅkar tuvāvimcati curutiyaippaṟṟic collum apippirāyattaiyum ārāycci ceytu, avaikaḷiṉ muṭivu terintu koṇṭu ataṉpiṉ karnāṭaka caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷaip pārkka vēṇṭum.
cāraṅkatēvaraip paṟṟiyum curutiyaip paṟṟi avar colliya
cūttiraṅkaḷaip paṟṟiyum cila kuṟippukaḷ
caṅkīta ratṉākaram eḻutiṉavar cāraṅkatēvar. ivar kāsmīra (Kashmir) tēcattil piṟantavar. varuṇakaṇariṣi kulattavar. ki.pi. 1210-m varuṣa mutal 1247-m varuṣam varai taulatapāt (near Aurangabad in Hyderabad) allatu tēvakiriyil āṇṭu koṇṭirunta cimmaṇarājaṉ (cōmarāja makārājaṉ) kālattiliruntavar. intac cōma rājāviṉuṭaiya kēṭṭuk koḷḷutaluk  kiṇaṅki innūl ceytatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivar tam kālattil vaḻaṅki vanta caṅkītattukkuriya amcaṅkaḷ yāvaiyum  camas kirutattil veku naṉṟāka eḻuti irukkiṟār. innūlil curutikaḷaip paṟṟit teriya virumpum yāvarum aṟiya vēṇṭiya viparam collap paṭuvatiṉālum, itu paḻamaiyāka eṇṇappaṭuvatiṉālum, curutikaḷaip paṟṟi ivar eṉṉa collukiṟār eṉṟu terintu koḷvatu mutal kaṭamaiyākum. ivar ceyta caṅkītaratṉākarattil cura attiyāyattil collappaṭum cila cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ karuttai uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭiyē moḻi peyarttu iṅku eḻutukiṟēṉ.
“iraṇṭu vīṇai tayār ceytu koḷ. ovvoṉṟukkum 22 tantikaḷ pōṭu. atil oru vīṇaiyil mutal tantiyil uṉṉāl kūṭiya ārampa nātam varumpaṭivai. ataṉ kīḻ vēṟu nātamirukkakkūṭātu. ataṉmēl koñcam kūṭutalāka 
2-m tantiyai amaittukkoḷ. iraṇṭu tantikkum naṭu mattiyil vēṟu nātam uṇṭākātapaṭiyirukkaṭṭum. itē pirakāramāka oṉṟiṉ mēloṉṟāy curuti cērttukkoḷ. ippaṭi mēl tantikaḷ pōkappōka oṉṟukkoṉṟu tīviramākum. ippaṭi iraṇṭum tayārceytukoḷ. atil ṣaṭjamam 4 curuti koṇṭatu. itil nālāvatu curutiyai ṣaṭjamamāka vaittuk koḷ. riṣapattiṟku 3 curuti, 5-vatu 6-vatu 7-vatu tantikaḷil riṣapam niṟkum. kāntārattiṟku 2 curuti, 8-vatu 9-vatu tantikaḷil varum. mattimattiṟku 4 curuti, 10, 11, 12, 13-vatu tantikaḷil niṟkum. pañcamattiṟku 4 curuti, 14, 15, 16, 17-vatu tantikaḷil toṉikkum. taivatattiṟku 3 curuti, 18, 19, 20-vatu tantikaḷil pēcum. niṣātattiṟku 2 curuti, atuvum 21, 22-l muṭikiṟatu. ivaṟṟil oṉṟu turuva vīṇai, maṟṟoṉṟu cala vīṇai eṉṟu vaittuk koḷ. ati cala vīṇaiyai nāṉ collukiṟapaṭi māṟṟu. 4-vatu ṣaṭjamattiṉ  piṉṉuḷḷa ṣaṭjamattiṉ 3-vatu curutiyiliruntu muṉ kiramappaṭi capta curaṅkaḷai vaittāl, oru curuti kuṟaiyum. iraṇṭāvatu 2 curuti kuṟaittuk koṇṭupōka ka vum ni yum riṣapa taivatattiṉ curutikaḷil oṉṟai aṭaiyum. mūṉṟāvatu 3 curuti kuṟaittuk koṇṭu pōka riṣapa taivatam ṣaṭjama pañcamattiṉ 4 curutiyaip peṟum. 4 curuti māṟṟumpōtu turuva vīṇaiyiluḷḷa ni, ka, ma vil cala vīṇaiyiṉ ca, ma, pa layattai aṭaikiṟatu. atāvatu 22-l ṣaṭjamamum 9-l mattimamum, 13-l pañcamamum ārampikkum. inta nāluvitam curuti kuṟaippatiṉāl turuva vīṇaiyiluḷḷa curaṅkaḷiṉ layattaiyaṭaikiṟatu. itiṉāl curaṅkaḷiṉ kaṇakku aṟiyappaṭum. ippaṭippaṭṭa curutikaḷilirun tum ṣaṭjam, riṣapam, kāntāram, mattimam, pañcamam, taivatam, niṣātam eṉṉum ēḻu curaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.”
ivviṭattil caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭaiya cūttiraṅkaḷai avaṟṟiluḷḷa paṭiyē arttam ceytirukkum kiḷameṇṭs (Mr. Clements) avarkaḷiṉ cila vākkiyaṅkaḷaiyum iṅkē eḻutuvatu nallateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
caṅkīta ratṉākarar tuvāvimcati curutikaḷaip paṟṟic colliya cila cūttiraṅkaḷukku Mr.. kiḷameṇṭs I.C.S. eḻutiya arttattiṉ moḻi peyarppu.
Indroduction to the Study of Indian Music by E. Clements P. 53.
THE SANGIT RATNAKAR
“Take two Vinas with 22 wires each and tune as follows. Let the first wire give the lowest possible note. The next a note a little higher and so on, so that between the notes given by any two adjacent wires a third note is impossible.
These successive notes are the Srutis. Sa will stand on the fourth wire, being a svara of four Srutis; ri will be on the third wire counting from the fifth; ga which has only two Srutis will fall on the second, counting from the eighth; ma being of four Srutis on the fourth, counting from the tenth; Pa on the fourth, counting from the fourteenth; Dha on the third after pa; ni on the second after Dha; so Ni will fall on the twenty-second Sruti.”
intiya caṅkītattiṟku mukavuraiyāka kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa pustakattiṉ 53- vatu pakkattil colliyirukkiṟatāvatu :- caṅkīta ratṉākaram cura attiyāyam 3-vatu pirakaraṇam 12-vatu culōkamutal 16-vatu culōkam varaiyil.
 “22 tantikaḷ pōṭṭa iraṇṭu vīṇai tayār ceytu koḷ. piṉ varukiṟapaṭi ataic curuticēr. mutal tanti kūṭiyavarai tāṭinanta cuttamāyirukkumpaṭi curuti cērttukkoḷ. aṭuttatu ataṟkuk koñcam kūṭutalāṉa curamāyirukkaṭṭum. ippaṭiyē maṟṟa 22 tantikaḷaiyum cērttukkoḷ. itil enta iraṇṭu tantikku naṭumattiyilum 3-vatu curam uṇṭākātapaṭi yirukkaṭṭum. ippaṭi oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāṉa curaṅkaḷ curutiyeṉṟaḻaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭic cērttukkoṇṭāl 4 curutikaḷaiyuṭaiya ṣaṭjama māṉatu 4-vatu tantiyil niṟkiṟatu. riṣapamāṉatu 5-vatu tantiyi liruntu 3 curutikaḷuṭaṉ amaikiṟatu. 2 curutikaḷaiyuṭaiya kāntāram 8, 9-vatu tantikaḷil varum. mattimam 4 curutikaḷai yuṭaiyatāy 10-vatu tanti mutal amaikiṟatu. 4 curutikaḷai yuṭaiya pañcamattai 14-vatu tanti mutal eṇṇip pōṭṭuk koḷ. 3 curutikaḷai yuṭaiya taivatattai pañcamattiṟku mēlākappōṭu. 2 curutikaḷai yuṭaiya niṣātattai taivatattiṟku mēl pōṭu ippaṭiyāṉāl 22-vatu iṭattil niṣātam varukiṟatu.”
mēlē kāṭṭiya iraṇṭuvitamāṉa moḻipeyarppum orē karuttuṭai yavaikaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu nāṉ nampukiṟēṉ. āṉāl itai vevvēṟuvitamāka arttam paṇṇikkoḷḷukiṟatiṉāl curuti kaḷuṭaiya niccayamum oṉṟāyirāmal palavāyirukkiṟatu. itōṭu caṅkīta pārijātak kārar eḻutiya muṟaippaṭi ceyyum curutikaḷaiyum mēṟṟicai caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷaiyum karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷaiyum kalantu tuvāvimcati curutikaḷ eṉṟa collaiyum viṭṭuviṭāmal, taṟkālattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷum keṭṭuppōkāmal irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉaittu pūrvīkam navīṉam eṉṉum iru muṟaikaḷaiyum niraval ceytu, ippaṭi vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟum ippaṭittāṉ nammuṭaiya kāṉam irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum anēka curuti muṟaikaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivvev vēṟu vitamāṉa apippirāyaṅkaḷaip pārkkiṟa evarukkum intiya caṅkītattiṉ paritāpamāṉa nilai teriyāmal pōkātu. makā paricuttameṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟa jalam eppaṭi intiyāvil kavaṉikkap paṭāmal pōkiṟatō, appaṭiyē intiya caṅkītamum nāḷaṭaivil kavaṉikkappaṭāmal maliṉa maṭaintatu. eṉṟālum pūrvamutal maṉaṉam ceytu maṉatil kāppāṟṟum mika uttamamāṉa muṟaiyiṉāl aṅkaṅku cila amcaṅkaḷ pēṇappaṭṭu, pūrvattai ñāpakappaṭutta viḷaṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷaik koṇṭu ippaṭiyirukka vēṇṭu meṉṟu rucuppaṭuttuvatu ilakuvāṉa kāriyamā yillai. āyiṉum, intiya caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷaip paṟṟi nāṉum cila vārttaikaḷ colla niṉaikkiṟēṉ. ‘nāṉ curutikaḷaip paṟṟic colvatu cariyāyirukkum, intiya caṅkītaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷiṉ vivakāram itōṭu muṭintuviṭu’ meṉṟu itaṉ muṉ eḻutiya kaṉavāṉkaḷ eṇṇiya paṭiyē nāṉum eṇṇiya eṇṇattai maṉṉikkak kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ. cilar mikunta ciramattuṭaṉ taṅkaḷ kālattaiyum poruḷaiyum celaviṭṭut taṅkaḷ apippirāyattai veḷiyiṭṭirukkum nūlkaḷāṉavai eṉ maṉatait tūṇṭiyatiṉimittamē nāṉum itaippaṟṟic cila vārttaikaḷ colla nēriṭṭatu. taṟkālattil, caṅkīta cāstiraṅkaḷil mikap paḻaimai yāṉatum, anēka viṣayaṅkaḷ aṭaṅki yiruppatumāṉa caṅkīta ratṉākarattil curutikaḷaip paṟṟic collum attiyāyattai, curutikaḷaip paṟṟi vicārikkum yāvarum mikunta kavaṉattōṭu paṭittiruppārkaḷ. avaikaḷil colliya curutikaḷ muṟṟilum vaḻakkattil illāmal oḻintupōyiṉaveṉpatu, curutikaḷaip paṟṟi eḻutiya kaṉavāṉkaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷaik koṇṭu tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu. ṣaṭjam, mēl ṣaṭjam eṉṟa iraṇṭu curaṅkaḷait tavira vēṟē entac curutiyāvatu caṅkīta ratṉākarattil colliyirukkum tuvāvimcati curuti kaṇakkukku ottirukkavillaiyeṉṟu kāṇkiṟēṉ. vekukālamāy vaḻaṅki vanta muṟaiyil cila cantēkaṅkaḷai nīkka niṉaittu ataiyē muṟṟilum māṟṟumpaṭiyāka niṉaippatu niyāyamalla. mēlum curutikaḷaippaṟṟi vevvēṟu vitamāy apippirāyam colluvataṟkuriya niyāyaṅkaḷ tiṭṭamāyc collappaṭāmaiyaiyum collappaṭṭavaikaḷum oru oḻuṅkai aṉucarikkappaṭavillai eṉpataiyum kāṇkiṟēṉ.
caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭaiya cūttiraṅkaḷukkuc cariyāka arttam paṇṇap paṭavillai eṉṟu naṉṟāp pulappaṭukiṟatu. vaṭatēcattil vaḻaṅki varum kāṉattiṉ mattiyilirukkum kaṉavāṉkaḷ curutiyaip paṟṟi eḻuti yirukkum vevvēṟu apippirāyaṅkaḷaip pārttāl, taṟkālattil vaḻaṅki varum kāṉam allatu caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭaiya apippirāyam eṉṟa iraṇṭil oṉṟu tavaṟutaluṭaiyatāyirukka vēṇṭum. mika nuṭpamāṉa oru muṟaiyai mikavum culapamāṉa vārttaikaḷāl coṉṉārēyoḻiya kaṭiṉamāṉa vārttaikaḷil avar collavillai. ellāraiyuṅ kāṇakkūṭiyatum, ellāruṅ kāṇakkūṭiyatumāṉa taṉatu kaṇṇai, oruvaṉ tāṉē kāṇā tirukkiṟatu eppaṭiyō appaṭiyē ellārukkum ilakuvāka viḷaṅkakkūṭiya ivviṣayamumirukkiṟatu. ivviṣayattil cāraṅkar muṟaippaṭi ceykiṟō meṉṟu cila amcaṅkaḷil ottu arttam paṇṇum cakastiraputti, rājā curēntira mōkaṉatākūr pōṉṟa mutal vakuppāraiyum, intustāṉ kītamuṟaippaṭi yeṉṟu arttam paṇṇum tēvāl, kiḷameṇṭs pōṉṟa iṭai vakuppāraiyum, mēṟṟicaic caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkum curaṅkaḷaiyum, maṟṟum avāntara curaṅkaḷaiyum kalantu itutāṉ karnāṭaka caṅkītam eṉṟu collum kaṭaivakuppāraiyum, avarkaḷ muṟaikaḷaiyum iṉṉavai yeṉṟu curukkamāka vicārittu ataṉpiṉ caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭaiya cariyāṉa apippirāyam iṉṉateṉṟu terintukoṇṭu, karnāṭaka caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkivarum curutikaḷaip pārppatu nallateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.








mutalāvatu.
I. cāraṅkar curuti muṟaippaṭi aḷavil ottirukkum mutal vakuppu.
1. cakastiraputti avarkaḷ colliyirukkum 22 curutiyiṉ muṟai.
taṟkālattil pūṉā kāyaṉa camājattiṉ Honorary Secretary yum nam intiya caṅkītam mikunta viruttiyaṭaiya vēṇṭumeṉṟu mikunta pirayācai eṭuttukkoṇṭavarumākiya cakastiraputti curutiyaip paṟṟi eḻutiya apippirāyam piṉvarumāṟu.
Hindu Music and the Gayan Samaj. Part ii. P. 13.
“If a monochord with moveable bridge be taken, and a space equal to 44 units be measured and the Bridge shifted to this point, the string when struck will yield a note; if we start with this note as the tonic or key-note and run through the Gamut by shifting the bridge (the Sanskrit writers affirm) the following facts will be observed. Sa will be produced at the distance 44; Ri at 40; Ga at 37; Ma at 35; Pa at 31; Dha at 27; Ni at 24; and Sa again at 22; but the latter Sa will be twice as intense as the former.”
“tanti oṉṟai eṭuttukkoṇṭu atil aṅkumiṅkum taḷḷi vaikkakkūṭiya oru ciṟu marappālamum vaittukkoḷvōm. ētāvatu oru nīḷattai eṭuttukkoṇṭu atai 44 caripaṅku kaḷākap pirittuk koṇṭu anta marappālattai ataṉ kaṭaici yil vaittut tantiyai mīṭṭiṉāl oru curam pēcukiṟatu eṉṟu vaittuk koḷḷuvōm. intac curattai ṣaṭjamamāka vaittuk koṇṭu, antap pālattai aṅkumiṅkum taḷḷuva tiṉāl oru stāyiyiluḷḷa curamellām terintupōkum eṉṟu kiranta nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. atu eppaṭi yeṉṟāl 44-vatu pirivil ṣaṭjamum 40-vatu pirivil riṣapamum, 37-vatil kāntāramum, 35-vatil mattimamum, 31-vatil pañcamamum, 27-vatil taivatamum, 24-vatil niṣātamum mēl stāyi ṣaṭjamam 22-vatilum pēcum. mēl stāyi ṣaṭjamam muntiṉa ṣaṭjamattai viṭa irupaṅku uḷḷatāyirukkum.”
ivar colliya kaṇakku ipputtakattaip pārkkum yāvarukkum naṉṟāy viḷaṅkum paṭi mutal 22 curutikaḷukku ivar koṭukkum aḷavaiyum ataṉpiṉ 32 aṅkulattil avaikaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷaiyum, ataṉ piṉ oru stāyiyil enta iṭattil avaikaḷ niṟkiṟateṉṟu kāṭṭum piṉṉattaiyum ātāra ṣaṭjam 540 vaiparēṣaṉāṉāl maṟṟa curaṅkaḷiṉ ōcaikaḷiṉ alaikaḷ ivvaḷaveṉpataiyum pirittukkāṭṭum aṭṭavaṇai itōṭu cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
kavaṉippu - curutikaḷaip paṟṟiya apippirāyam palapalavāyum avar kaḷuṭaiya aḷavum, ōcaikaḷiṉ alaikaḷum vevvēṟāyumiruppatiṉāl, ottup pārppatu caṅkaṭam. ākaiyāl ellōrum ilakuvāy aṟintu koḷḷak kūṭiyatāka tantiyiṉ nīḷam 32 aṅkulamākavum ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ 540 āyum vaittuk koṇṭu avaravarkaḷ kaṇakkukku māṟṟi yirukkiṟēṉ.
mēlum 22 curutikaḷ cērkkum muṟai coṉṉa cāraṅkatēvaruṭaiya muṟaikku itu muṟṟilum ovvātatāyiruntālum maṟṟavaikaḷōṭu ottup pārppataṟku aṉukūlamāka ovvoru curutikkum ittaṉai ceṉṭskaḷ eṉṟum avaikaḷ ovvoṉṟukkumuḷḷa pētam iṉṉateṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷakkūṭiya kaṇakkum itōṭu cērttirukkiṟēṉ. itai vācikkiṟavarkaḷ culapamāy ottuppārttukkoḷḷak kūṭumātalāl avai collāmal viṭappaṭṭaṉa.









1-vatu aṭṭavaṇai.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavai yeṉṟu makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cakastiraputti avarkaḷ apippirāyattaik kāṭṭum tuvāvimcati curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇai

* ivvaṭaiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavai.

kuṟippu - ivar tantiyiṉ nīḷam 44 eṇkaḷ eṉṟu vaittuk koṇṭu, atiṉ mattiya pākattil tārastāy ṣaṭjam pirikkiṟār. itu yāvarum ilakuvāy aṟintu koḷḷak kūṭiyatē. tāra ṣaṭjattiṟkuk kīḻ uḷḷa mattiyastāy 22 eṇkaḷ nīḷamuḷḷatākiṟatu. inta 22 eṇkaḷil ovvoru eṇṇukku ovvoru curutiyāka 22 curutiyākiṟatu. tiruṣṭāntamāka 44 eṇkaḷiṉ nīḷattaiyum 44 aṅkulameṉṟu vaittuk koḷvōmēyāṉāl ovvoru aṅkulattukkum ovvoru curutiyāka 22-vatu curutiyil mattiyastāyi amaikiṟatu. ivar ṣaṭjattiṟkuriya 4 curutikaḷil mutal curutiyai ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vaittuk koḷḷukiṟār. 44-l iruntu 41 varai ṣaṭjamattiṟkuriya 4 curuti kaḷākavum 40, 39, 38 riṣapattiṟkuriya 3 curutikaḷākavum 37, 36 kāntārattuk kuriya 2 curutikaḷākavum 35, 34, 33, 32 mattimattiṟkuriya 4 curutikaḷākavum 31, 30, 29, 28 pañcamattiṟkuriya 4 curutikaḷāka vum 27, 26, 25 taivatattiṟkuriya 3 curutikaḷākavum 24, 23 niṣātattiṟkuriya 2 curuti kaḷākavum varukiṟatu. ākavē, ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 3, kāntāram 2, mattiyamam 4, pañcamam 4, taivatam 3, niṣātam 2 āka 22 curutikaḷ eṉṟu collukiṟār. itil nām kavaṉikkakkūṭiyatu oṉṟu uṇṭu. cāraṅka tēvar caṅkītaratṉā karattil uṉṉāl kuṟainta ōcaiyai mutal tantiyil vaittukkoḷ. ataṟku mēl varakkūṭiya ōcaiyai iraṇṭām tantiyil vaittukkoḷ. iraṇṭukkum naṭuvil vēṟu ōcaiyuṇṭākak kūṭātu. ippaṭip paṭip paṭiyāka ōcaikaḷaik kūṭṭik koṇṭu pōka avaikaḷ tīviramākumeṉṟu ōcaiyaik kuṟittuc coṉṉārē yoḻiyat tantikaḷiṉ aḷavil avaikaḷ ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu collavillai yeṉṟu nām mukkiyamāyk kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. itaṟku arttam paṇṇiṉa cakastira putti eṉpavar caṅkīta ratṉākarar mantarastāyi ōcai oru paṅkāṉāl mattiyastāy iru maṭaṅkum, tārastāy mattiya stāyiyiṉum iru maṭaṅkumāka irukkumeṉṟu colliyirukkiṟatai aṟivār eṉṟu niṉaikkiṟēṉ. appaṭiyāṉāl mattiya stāyikkuḷḷa eṇkaḷil atāvatu 22 aṅkulattil atu muṭivākiṟa tāṉāl tārastāy 11 aṅkulattōṭu muṭivaṭaiya vēṇṭum. ippaṭiyē mēl pōkappōka, aḷaviṉ eṇkaḷ pāti pātiyākak kuṟaintum ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ iraṇṭu iraṇṭu maṭaṅkāka mēl kūṭiyum pōkiṟateṉṟu aṟiyalām. itaip pōlavē oru stāyi yiluḷḷa curaṅkaḷum paṭippaṭiyākak kūṭutalāki iraṇṭil muṭivaṭaikiṟatu. oru tantiyiṉ pātikkukkīḻāka mattiya stāyiyum pātikkumēl nālil oṉṟil tāra stāyiyum eṭṭil oṉṟil atitārastāyiyum jiyā meṭrikal purōkiraṣaṉpaṭi (Geometrical Progression) pēcukiṟatai nām yāvarum aṟivōm. itupōlavē, oru stāyi yiluḷḷa ovvoru curamum paṭippaṭiyākat tantiyiṉ nīḷattil jiyā meṭṭirikal purōkiraṣaṉpaṭi kuṟaintu koṇṭē pōkiṟatu. tāra ṣaṭjattiṉ mēluḷḷa riṣapam mattiya stāyiyiṉ riṣapattiṉ aḷavil pātiyākak kuṟainta teṉṟum mattiya stāyiyiṉ riṣapam tārastāyi yiṉ riṣapattiṉ tanti aḷaviṟku 2 maṭaṅku atikamāṉateṉṟum nām pārkkalām. āṉāl tārastāyiṣaṭjattiṉ kīḻuḷḷa niṣātam mēluḷḷa riṣapattiṉ aḷavukku cumār 9l oṉṟu kūṭiyirukkiṟatu. tantiyiṉ aḷavil riṣapam ippaṭik kuṟaintiruntālum ōcaiyiṉ aḷavil riṣapam cumār 8l oṉṟu kūṭi yirukkiṟatākak kāṇpōm. ippaṭiyē capta curaṅkaḷum oru stāyiyil mēl pōkappōka ōcaiyil kūṭiyum tantiyil kuṟaintumirukka vēṇṭi yatu. appaṭi illāmal camamākat tantiyai aḷaviṭum pōtu ōcaiyil vēṟupaṭṭirukkum. atāvatu caṅkīta ratṉākarar colliya ōcaiyiṉpaṭi atu ottirukkamāṭṭātu. muṭivāka ōcaiyiṉ aḷavil oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu pēta miṉṟi oṟṟumaiyāy, orē aḷavāy mēlē pōka vēṇṭumeṉṟu coṉṉa cāraṅkatēvar apip pirāyattiṟku, tantiyiṉ aḷavil oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu pēta miṉṟi ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa cakastiraputti avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam muṟṟilum ōcaiyil ovvātu eṉṟu nāṉ niṉaikkiṟēṉ. āṉāl nāta māṉatu cama aḷavāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa apippirāyam ivaruk kuṇṭāṉatai nām mikavum kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. ivar tantiyil eṭuttuk koṇṭa aḷavaiyum, cāraṅkatēvar eṭuttukkoṇṭa aḷavaiyum itiṉ piṉvarum aṭṭavaṇaiyil kāṇalām. appaṭiyē ivar eṭuttuk koṇṭa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi varum ōcaiyiṉ aḷavukkaṇakkaiyum cāraṅkatēvar eṭuttuk koṇṭa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi varum ōcaiyiṉ aḷavukkaṇakkaiyum itiṉ piṉvarum aṭṭavaṇai yil pārkkalām.
ivar oru stāyiyai 22 pākaṅkaḷākap pirittu atil mutal nālu curutiyaiyum ṣaṭjamattukkuriya teṉkiṟār. āṉāl ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālāvatu curuti mēruviṉiṭamākap pēcukiṟatai nām yāvarum aṟivōm.






iraṇṭāvatu.
rājā curēntira mōkaṉa tākōr avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutiyiṉ muṟai.
ivar kalkattāvāci. nam intiya caṅkītattiṟku utaviyāyirukkum vāttiyaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟaic cēkarittu pārīs nakarattiṉ kaṇkāṭcikku aṉuppi, intiya caṅkītam mēṟṟicai yārukkum viḷaṅkum vaṇṇam anēka nūlkaḷ ceytavar. 1875-m varuṣattil nam intiya cakkaravarttiṉi avarkaḷ pērālum avarkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ pērālum “vikṭōriya kītikā” eṉṟa caṅkīta pustakam eḻutiṉavar. intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷaik kāṇpataṟkāka aṭiyil varum vivarappaṭi pirittirukkiṟār. ataippaṟṟi ivar eḻutiya pustakam eṉakkuk kiṭaikkāviṭṭālum tēvāl eḻutiyirukkiṟataik koṇṭu ivaratu curuti muṟaiyaic collukiṟēṉ.
Hindu Musical Scale and 22 Srutis by K.B. Deval P. 34.
Mr. Deval says that he (Raja Surendra Mohan Tagore) divided the whole speaking length of the wire into two halves, the whole length giviing the sa or fundamental note and the half giving this sa the octave; both these notes are correct. Again he divided the first half into two equal parts each being 1/4 of the whole length. The first quarter of the wire he subdivided into 9 equal parts calling each part a Sruti. And at the end of the 9th part is sounded a note ma (at 1/4 of the wire) which is correct. In the next quarter of the wire he made 13 equal subdivisions each being also called a Sruti. Thus in all he got the 22 Srutis.
 “atāvatu oru tantiyai iraṇṭu camapākamākap pirikkiṟār. muḻut tantiyil ātāra ṣaṭjam pēcukiṟatu. ataṉ pātiyil tāra ṣaṭjam pēcum. inta nōṭṭukaḷ cariyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. mutal pātiyai iraṇṭu camapākamākap pirikkiṟār. atil ov voṉṟum mottattil nālil oṉṟākiṟatu. mutalāvatu 1/4 paṅku tantiyai 9 camapākaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟār. atil ov voṉṟum oru curutiyeṉṟu collukiṟār. atil oṉpatāvatu pākattil muṭikiṟa curuti ma veṉṟu aḻaikkap paṭum. atuvum cariyāyirukkiṟatu. tantiyiṉ aṭutta kāl pākattai 13 cama pākaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟār. itaṉ ovvoru pākamum ovvoru curuti eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. āka mottattil 22 curutikaḷum kiṭaittirukkiṉṟaṉa.”
ṣaṭjamum, mattimamum cariyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu tēvāl tam muṭaiya pustakam 34, 35-m pakkaṅkaḷil collukiṟār. itaik kavaṉikkai yil oru stāyiyai 22 curutikaḷākap pirikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avar apip pirāyappaṭukiṟār eṉpatu tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu.
āṉāl cakastiraputtiyavarkaḷuṭaiya apippirāyattiṟkum, ivarkaḷu ṭaiya apippirāyattiṟkum koñcam vittiyācamiruppatāka nām aṟiyalām. atāvatu, tantiyiṉ nālil oṉṟai 9 pākamākavum atiṉ mēluḷḷa nālil oṉṟai 13 camapākaṅkaḷākavum vakuttatē. mottattil camapākaṅkaḷeṉṟu niṉaittatu cariyāyiruntālum, curastāṉaṅkaḷil caṟṟup pētamiruppatāka nām pārkkalām. oru stāyiyai 22 camapākaṅkaḷākap pirikkaiyil caka stira puttiyiṉ apippirāyattiṉpaṭi 12-vatu curuti anta stāyikku matti yākiṟatu. appōtu 10-vatu curutiyākiya mattimattiṟkumēl 2 curutikaḷ cērntu varum. atāvatu mattimattiṉuṭaiya 4 curutikaḷil 3vatu curutimatti yākum. āṉāl rājā curēntiramōkaṉa tākōr apippirāyattilō oru stāyiyiṉ mattiyapākamāṉatu 9 curutikaḷuṭaiya mattimam ākumeṉṟu collukiṟār. cakastiraputtiyavarkaḷ mattiyastāyiyai caripākaṅkaḷākap pirikkaiyil 12-vatu iṭam ippōtu nām cātāraṇamāyc collik koḷḷukiṟa mattimamākiṟatu. atāvatu 3/4 ākiṟatu. ivar kaṇakkiṉpaṭi atu mūṉṟāvatu mattimam ākum. āṉāl ivar 10-vatākac collum mutal mattimamāṉatu tantiyiṉ caripātiyil varukiṟatu. iraṇṭu curutikaḷ cakastira putti collum aṭṭavaṇaikkuk kuṟaintu varukiṟatākat terikiṟatu. matti mattiṉ nālu curutikaḷil mutalāvatu curuti tantiyiṉ pātiyil varukiṟa teṉṟu ivarum 3-vatu curuti tantiyiṉ caripātiyil varukiṟateṉṟu cakastira puttiyum collukiṟatai nām kavaṉikka vēṇṭum.
tantiyiṉ nūlil oru pākattil mattimam caṟṟu muṉpiṉ varukiṟatā yiruntālum ataṟkuk kīḻ camamāka 9 paṅkukaḷ paṇṇavēṇṭu meṉṟum ataṟkumēl camamāka 13 paṅkukaḷ paṇṇavēṇṭumeṉṟum avar colla villai. mēlum mantara mattiya tārastāyikaḷ tantiyiṉ aḷavil oṉṟu, arai, kālāka mēl pākap pōkak kuṟukip pōvatu pōl oru stāyi yikkuḷ varukiṟa curaṅkaḷum vara vēṇṭumēyoḻiya camapākaṅkaḷāka varavēṇṭiya niyāyamillai. eṉṟālum, ōcaikaḷ camapākamāy varavēṇṭu meṉṟa apippirāyattukkup patil curutikaḷ cama aḷavāṉa tantiyil varavēṇṭumeṉṟu kuṟittatāṉatu nām kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyatu. ivar oru tantiyiṉ, nālil oru pākattai 9 camapākaṅkaḷākavum ataṟkumēl nālil oru pākattai 13 cama pākaṅkaḷākavum pirikkumpaṭic collukiṟār. appaṭip pirikkumpoḻutu 32 aṅkulat tantiyil iṉṉiṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷil curutikaḷ varukiṟateṉṟum ātāra ṣaṭjattiṉ ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ 540-l iruntu kūṭutal ivvaḷavu varukiṟateṉṟum kāṭṭakkūṭiya aṭṭavaṇai etir pakkattil kāṇka.
inta aṭṭavaṇaiyaiyum cakastiraputti aṭṭavaṇaiyaiyum ottup pārp pōmēyāṉāl ātāra ṣaṭjattaiyum tāraṣaṭjattaiyum tavira vēṟu enta curaṅkaḷum ottirukka māṭṭātu. pañcamamum mattimamumē ottirukkā viṭṭāl nām itil collakkūṭiyatu eṉṉa irukkiṟatu? oru stāyiyil kiṭaikkakkūṭiya inta iraṇṭu curaṅkaḷum mika mukkiyamāṉavai. caṅkītat til ivviraṇṭu curaṅkaḷumillātirukku māṉāl ṣaṭjamattiṟku vēṟu poruttamuḷḷa curam illāmaṟ pōkum. maṟṟu enta curaṅkaḷ vittiyācap paṭṭālum mattima pañcamaṅkaḷ oṟṟumai yāyirukka vēṇṭum. caṅkīta ratṉākār ippaṭic collavillai. mikat teḷivākac colli yirukkiṟār. āṉālum itaiyē iṉṉum anēkar vevvēṟu vita arttam ceytiruk kiṟārkaḷ. avaikaḷil cilavaṟṟai oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāyp pārppōm.
ivar oru stāyiyai 22 pākaṅkaḷākap pirittu atil mutal nālu curutiyaiyum ṣaṭjamattukkuriyateṉkiṟār. āṉāl ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālāvatu curuti mēruviṉiṭamākap pēcukiṟatai nām aṟivōm.
maṟṟavai yāvum iṅkē colvatu avaciyamalla. āṉāl, caṅkīta ratṉākarar ippaṭippaṭṭa paṅkuvītam collavillai.

2-vatu aṭṭavaṇai.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavai yeṉṟu
rājā curēntira mōkaṉa tākōr avarkaḷ
apippirāyattaik kāṭṭum tuvāvimcati curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇai.
caṅkīta ratṉākara muṟaippaṭi.

* ivvaṭaiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavai

mūṉṟāvatu
II. cāraṅkar curuti muṟaippaṭi intustāṉi kītam irukkiṟateṉṟu collum iraṇṭām vakuppu.
K.B. tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutiyiṉ muṟai.
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl (Mr. Deval) teṟku makārāṣṭira tēcattaic cērnta caṅkili eṉṉumūriluḷḷavar. ivar intiya caṅkītattil oru stāyiyil 22 curutikaḷ irukkiṟateṉṟu oru nūl eḻutiyirukkiṟār. atil 22 curutikaḷ ceyyum vitattaip paṟṟi eḻutiya vitikaḷ patiṉmūṉṟum piṉvarumāṟu :-
Sa1 (C1) Sa2 (C2)
1. “The whole length f the wire between the two fixed bridges gives the Fundamental Note. Sa1 (C1). Let the length of the wire be 36 inches, the note produced be called Sa1 (C1) and let its vibrations be 240 per second.
2. The note produced on half the length is in value equal to Sa1 (C1) and in pitch or vibrations it is double the Fundamental Note.
The note produced on the length 18” is therefore Sa1 (C1) itself, but one octave higher. Let this note be called Sa2 (C2) to distinguish it from Sa1 (C1) the F.N. ; then the vibrations of Sa2 (C2) are double i.e. 2 x 240 = 480 per second.
3. The pitch of a note or its vibrations are inversely proportional to the length of the wire.
This rule is a legitimate inference from the above two rules. Rule (1) permits us to take any length for the Fundamental Note (F.N.) and according to rule (2) if the length is halved the pitch is doubled, and if the length is doubled the pitch or the number of vibrations is halved. If therefore 1/3 length is taken the pitch or the number of vibrations produced will be trebled. Or by generalization :
4. The pitch varies inversely as the length and vice versa.
The above four rules may therefore be put in the form of a simple Formula for convenience of working and ready reference.
Let V0 = vibrations or pitch of the note on wire Ln inches long. Un = The vibrations or pitch of Sa1 (C1) the F.N. here = 240.
L = the length of the wire of the F.N. here = 36 inches = 36”
Then :
Vn x ln = U x l 	..... ..... 	(A)
	Vn = U x l/ln 	..... ..... 	(B)
	ln = U x l/Vn 	..... ..... 	(C)
therefore if Vn = 2 U
	ln = l/2	..... ..... 	(D)
Ma (F)
5. The note Ma (or the fourth note) is produced at the middle of the Fundamental Note and its octave.
The note Ma is therefore produced at half the length of Sa1 (C1 ) and Sa2 (C2 ) or at 1/2 (36” + 18”) = 1/2 (54”) = 27”. In other words the note of the wire 27 inches or 27” of the executive part of the wire will give out the 4th note or Ma (F) and by rule (4) formula (B) the pitch or vibrations of Ma (F) are equal to 320.
The formula (B) is :
Vn = U x l/ln; Here U = 240, l = 36 and ln = 27.
Vn = 240 x 36/27 = 320 = Vibrations of Ma.
And Formula (C) is :
ln = U x l/Vn  ~ ln = 240 x l/320 = 3/4 l = 3/4 x 36
or the length of Ma is 3/4 of the length of the F.N. and the Vibrations of Ma are 4/3 of the F.N. and it may be laid down :-
6. That the length of the wire of Ma (F) or the 4th note is 3/4 of that of the Fundamental Note and the Vibrations of Ma (F) are 4/3 of the Vibrations of the Fundamental Note Sa1 (C1).
Pa (G)
7. The fifth or Pa (G) note is produced on 1/3 or 2/3 of whole length of the wire. The former note is one octave higher than the latter.
The length of the wire is 36”. Therefore a length of 12” or 24” will give the fifth note Pa (G). But we want the length between 18” and 36” - the two limits of the octave. Therefore the length 24” is that which we require and it will give out the note Pa (G).
Let us apply the formulae (B) and (C) to the case of Pa (G)
	Vn = U x l/ln 	..... ..... 	(B)
Substitute the values U = 240, l = 36 and ln = 24
	Vn = 240 x 36/24 = 360 = Vibrations of Pa (G) = U x 36/24 = 3/2 U
	or the vibrations of Pa (G) are 3/2 of its Sa1 or F.N.
and
	ln = U x l/Vn 	..... ..... 	(C)
	~ ln = 240/360 x l = 2/3 l = 24.
	or the length of Pa is 2/3 of its Sa1 (C1) or F.N.
	These facts may be noted down under the rule (8) below.
8. The length of the wire of Pa (G) or the fifth note is 2/3 of that of Sa1’s (C1 ) wire and its vibrations or pitch is 3/2 of that of Sa1 (C1 ).
Ri (D)
9. In the interval of a given octave Sa1 (C1 ) with Pa (G) and Ma (F) with Sa2 (C2 ) form perfect concords; it may be noted that Sa1 (C1 ) with Ma (F) and Pa (G) with Sa2 (C2 ), the inverted interval, form imperfect concords.
This rule is very important and is made use of in finding out the lengths and vibrations of the other notes Ri (D), Ga (E), etc., etc.
According to Rule (1) any length may be said to give the fundamental note and its Pa will be the 5th note from it. This Pa will form a perfect concord with it. This gives us the following consonant notes.
F.N. Sa1 (C1) 	consonant note 	Pa or Ma (F)
Sa1 C1			”	Pa G
Ri D			”	Dha A
Ga E			”	Ni B
Ma F			”	Sa2 C2
Pa G			”	Ri2 D2
Sa2 C2			”	Pa2 G2 or Pa G.
Let us take Pa (G) itself as the starting or fundamental note; then its Pa or fifth will be D in the higher octave which may be called Ri2 (D2 ). Apply the formula (B)
Vn = U l/ln ; here U = 360, l = 24, and ln = 2/3 x 24; ~ ln = 16.
Rule 7 Substituting the values of U, l and ln
Vn = 360 x 24/16 = 540.
540 are the vibrations of Ri2 or Ri (D) in the 2nd octave. Therefore the vibrations of Ri in the first octave are = 1/2 x 540 = 270 Vide Rule (2).
Formula C is - ln = U x 1/Vn; substituting U = 240, l = 36 and Vn = 270.
We have ln = 240 x 36/270 = 32. Hence -
10. The length of Ri (D) is 32 inches and its vibrations are 270; or, the length is 8/9 l, and vibrations 9/8 U.
Dha (A)
11. The length of Ri is 32”. If we take this as the starting note then its Pa is Dha. Therefore the length of Dha is 2/3 of 32 = 211/3 by Rule 8 and its vibrations are 3/2 of 270 = 405.
Ga (E)
The length of Dha (A) is 211/3 and its vibrations are 405. Let us take Dha as the fundamental note (Rule 1); then Ga2 (E) or Ga in 2nd octave becomes its Pa or the 5th note (Rule 9). Therefore its length is 2/3 x 211/3 and vibrations 3/2 x 405; but these are for Ga2. Therefore according to Rule (2) the length of Ga is = 2 x 2/3 x 211/3 = 2 x 2/3 x 64/3 = 256/9 = 284/9
and the vibrations of Ga = 1/2 x 3/2 x 405 = 1215/4 = 3033/4.
Ni (B)
The length of Ga is 284/9 and its vibrations 3033/4 (Rule 12). If we take Ga as the Fundamental note, then Ni becomes its Pa or the fifth note. Therefore.
the length of Ni = 2/3 x 284/5 = 2/3 x 256/9 = 512/27 = 1826/27
and the vibrations of Ni = 3/2 x 3033/4 = 3/2 x 1215/4 = 3645/8 = 4555/8
Ga (E)
Again
12. If the vibrations of Ga (E) be taken as 300 (and there is a reason for doing so) in place of
303 3/4 as obtained in Rule (2) above, then
ln = Ul/Vn  	(C); U = 240, l = 36 and Vn = 300.
~   ln = 240 x 36/300 = 144/5 = 284/5.
Hence the length of Ga = 284/5” and its pitch = 300.
The Ga (E) obtained by the foregoing process has 3033/4 vibrations and bears with the F.N. a complicated ratio viz 81 : 64. The Ga (E) obtained as the fifth harmonic when reduced by two octaives has 300 vibrations and bears with the F.N. the simple ratio of 5 : 4; and it sounds more consonant with it. It is clearly heard on the bass string (the fourth, giving Sa or F.N.) of the Vina. Sanskrit writers have adopted this in preference to the other. They tested their notes by harmonics; the author of Ragavibodha clearly lays down this.
Ni (B)
13. If Ga (E) is taken as the Fundamental Note, then Ni (B) becomes its Pa the fifth in the same octave.
~ 300 x 3/2 = 450 = the vibrations of Ni by Rule (8);
and the length is 2/3 x 284/5 = 2/3 x 144/5 = 288/15 = 193/15 = 191/5;
i.e. The vibrations of Ni = 450
and the length of Ni = 191/5. Etc. Etc.
ca1, ca2
1. oru tanti pōṭṭa oru tampuruvil mēruvukkum meṭṭukkum naṭumattiyiluḷḷa tanti oṉṟeṉṟu vaittuk koḷ. itai mīṭṭiṉāl ātāra ṣaṭjam pēcum.
Notes - antat tantiyiṉ nīḷam 36 aṅkula mirukkaṭṭum. itil pēcum curam ca1 (C1) eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭum. atiṉuṭaiya vaiparēṣaṉ allatu ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ 240 āyirukkaṭṭum.
2. antat tantiyiṉ caripātiyil ātāraṣaṭjam (C1) pōla oru ṣaṭjam pēcukiṟatu. ataṉuṭaiya stāyiyil allatu ōcai yiṉ aḷavil ātāra ṣaṭjat tiṟku 2 maṭaṅkāyirukkiṟatu. (C2)
itu tantiyiṉ 18 aṅkulattil pēcuvatiṉāl ca1 (C1) eṉṟu collappaṭum. āṉāl oru stāyi mēlāyirukkiṟatu.
itai ca2 (C2)  eṉṟu colluvōm.
itu 480 vaiparēṣaṉāyirukkum.
3. oru curattiṉ ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ allatu vaiparēṣaṉ kiramamāyt tantiyiṉ aḷavukku māṟutalāka varukiṟatu.
inta viti itaṟku muntiṉa iraṇṭu vitikaḷiṉ niyāyattai aṉucarittuc collappaṭukiṟatu.
mutal viti tanti enta aḷavāyiruntālum atil ātāra ṣaṭjam pēcum eṉṟu collukiṟatu.
iraṇṭāvatu viti antat tantiyai iraṇṭu camapākamākap pirittāl ōcaiyāṉatu atil iru maṭaṅkākiṟateṉṟum antat tantiyai 2 paṅku nīḷam ceytāl atiṉ ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ pātiyākiṟateṉṟum collukiṟatu.
ākaiyiṉāl tantiyiṉ mūṉṟil oru pākattai eṭuttuk koṇṭāl anta vaiparēṣaṉ mūṉṟu maṭaṅku atikamākum. allatu potuvāka :
4. ōcaiyāṉatu tantiyiṉ aḷavukku māṟutalāka muṟaiyē māṟukiṟatu.
tantiyum appaṭiyē ōcaiyiṉ aḷavukku māṟutalāka varukiṟatu.
mēṟkaṇṭa nāluvitikaḷum iṉimēl ceyyappōkiṟa vēlai kaḷukku aṉukūlamāyum appōtaikkappōtu ottiṭṭup pārppataṟku ilakuvāyum irukkumpaṭip piramāṇamāka vaittuk koḷvōm.
Vn = lnil pēcappaṭum vaiparēṣaṉ allatu ōcaiyāyirukkaṭṭum. Un =  ātāra ṣaṭjattiṉ allatu C1 iṉ 240 vaiparēṣaṉukkuc cari.
L = ātāra ṣaṭjam piṟakkum tantiyiṉ nīḷam = 36 aṅkulam iṅkē vaittuk koṇṭōm.
ākaiyāl –
Vn x ln = U x l 	..... ..... 	(A)
Vn = U x l/ln 	..... ..... 	(B)
ln = U x l/Vn 	..... ..... 	(C)
ākaiyāl Vn = 2 U camāṉamāṉāl
ln = 1/2	..... ..... 	(D)
ma
5. nālāvatu curamākiya mattimam (F)  ātāra ṣaṭjattukkum tāra ṣaṭjattukkum naṭumattiyil uṇṭākum.
atāvatu meṭṭiliruntu 27 aṅkulattil pēcukiṟatu.
muṉ coṉṉa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi ataṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ 240x36/27 = 320.
6. nālāvatu curamākiya mattimam niṟkum tantiyiṉ nīḷam ātāra ṣaṭjam pēcum tantiyiṉ nīḷattiṟku 3/4 ākiṟatu. matti mattiṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ ātāra ṣaṭjamattiṉ vaiparēṣaṉukku 4/3 ākiṟatu.
pa
7. aintāvatu curamākiya pañcamam G.) tantiyiṉ motta nīḷattil 1/3 allatu 2/3 l pēcukiṟatu.
1/3 l pēcukiṟa pañcamam 2/3l pēcappaṭum pañcamattiṟku 2 maṭaṅku.
tantiyiṉ nīḷam 36 aṅkulam.
itil 12 aṅkulattilum 24 aṅkulattilum pañcamam pēcukiṟatu.
ippōtu tantiyiṉ motta nīḷattiṟkum atāvatu 36 aṅkulat tiṟkum atiṉ pātiyākiya 18 aṅkulattiṟkum naṭu mattiyil enta iṭam eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikka vēṇṭum.
pañcamam 24 aṅkulattilum 12 aṅkulattilum irukkiṟatu eṉṟu colliyirukkiṟōm. 24 aṅkulattilirukkum pañcamam ippoḻutu namakku vēṇṭum.
muṉcoṉṉa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi Vn  = 240 x 36/24 = 360.
atāvatu - 240 x 3/2 = 360.
8. pañcamam niṟkum tantiyiṉ nīḷam ṣaṭjamam pēcum tantiyiṉ nīḷattiṟku 2/3, appaṭiyē ataṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ ṣaṭjamattiṉ ōcaiyiṉ aḷavukku 3/2 āy irukka vēṇṭum.
ri
9. ippōtu kiṭaitta oru stāyiyil ṣaṭjamamum pañcamamum, mattimamum ṣaṭjamum pūraṇamāṉa oṟṟumai yuṭaiyavaikaḷ.
nām kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyatāvatu, ātāra ṣaṭjamamum mattimamum, pañcamamum ca2vum koñcam kuṟainta oṟṟumai yuṭaivaikaḷ. inta viti mikavum ri, ka mutaliya curaṅkaḷiṉ aḷavaiyum, ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷaiyum kaṇṭupiṭippataṟku mikavum upayōka muḷḷatu.
mutal vitiyiṉpaṭi enta nīḷamāṉa tantiyilum ātāra ṣaṭjam piṟakkum. atiliruntu aintāvatu curam pañcamamākum. inta pañcamam, ṣaṭjamattōṭu oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyatu. itu piṉṉāl varum curaṅkaḷaik koṭukkiṟatu.
ca1 		pa
ri 		ta
ka 		ni
ma		ca2
pa 		ri2
ca2 		pa2
eppaṭiyeṉṟāl :- pañcamattai ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vaittuk koḷvōm. atiliruntu ataṉ 5vatu curammēl stāyiyil pañcamamākum. atu mēl stāyiyiṉ ri2 ākum. mēl colliya kaṇakkiṉpaṭi atiṉ ōcaiyiṉ aḷavu 540. tantiyiṉ aḷavu 16 aṅkulamākum. ippōtu riṣapattiṉ ōcai yiṉ aḷavukaḷ 540. appaṭiyāṉāl kīḻ stāyiyiṉ riṣapattiṉ aḷavu atil pātiyākiya 270 āka irukka vēṇṭum. 240 vaiparēṣa ṉukku 36 aṅkula tantiyāṉāl, 270 vaiparēṣaṉukku
240 x 36/270 = 32 ākum. ākavē
10. riṣapam 32 aṅkulattil pēcukiṟatu. atiṉuṭaiya vaiparēṣaṉ 270. allatu intat tantiyiṉuṭaiya nīḷam ṣaṭjama tanti nīḷattiṉ 8/9 ākiṟatu. atiṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ aḷavu 9/8 ākiṟatu.
ta
11. riṣapattiṉ tantiyiṉ nīḷam 32 aṅkulam. itai ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vaittukkoṇṭāl itaṉ 5vatu curam pa vākiya taivatamākum. ākaiyiṉāl taivatam tantiyiṉ nīḷam 32 aṅkulattil 2/3. atāvatu taivatam ṣaṭjama tantiyil 2/3 x 32 = 211/3 aṅkulattil varukiṟatu. atiṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ = 3/2 x 270 = 405.
ka
ākavē taivatam tantiyiṉ 211/3 aṅkulattil pēcukiṟatu. atiṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ 405. ippōtu taivatattai ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vaittukkoḷvōm. appaṭiyāṉāl iraṇṭāvatu stāyiyiluḷḷa ka2 taivatattiliruntu 5vatu curamākum. atiṉ aḷavu 2/3 x 211/3 = 2/3 x 64/3 = 128/9 = 142/9
ataṉ ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ = 3/2 x 405 = 6071/2. itu mēl stāyiyiluḷḷa kāntārattiṟkāka. ākaiyiṉāl 2vatu vitiyiṉpaṭi kāntārattiṉuṭaiya nīḷam 2 x 142/9 = 284/9. itu ātāra ṣaṭjamattiṟku mēl varum kāntāram. itiṉ ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ = 1/2 x 6071/2 = 3033/4.
ippōtu kāntārattiṟkut tantiyiṉ nīḷam = 281/9. atiṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ = 3033/4.
ni
kāntārattai ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vaittukkoḷḷuvōmē yāṉāl ataṉu ṭaiya 5vatu curam ni yākum. ākaiyiṉāl niṣātattiṉ nīḷam = 2/3 x 284/9 = 2/3 x 256/9 = 512/27 = 1826/27.
appaṭiyāṉāl niṣātattiṉ ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ
= 3/2 x 3033/4 = 3/2 x 1215/4 = 3645/8 = 4555/8.
maṟupaṭiyum
ka
12. kāntārattiṉ ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ 3033/4kkup patil 300 eṉṟu eṭuttuk koḷvōmōyāṉāl (appaṭi eṭuttukkoḷḷukiṟa taṟku niyāyamumirukkiṟatu) muṉ coṉṉa cūttirattiṉpaṭi atiṉuṭaiya tantiyiṉ nīḷam 284/5 ākum. muṭivāka kāntāram niṟkum tantiyiṉ aḷavu 284/5. ataṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ 300.
itaṟku muṉ nam cūttirattiṉpaṭi kāntārattiṟku 3033/4 ōcai yiṉ alaikaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. ṣaṭjamattiliruntu niṣātat tiṟku 81/64 āka varukiṟatu. kāntāram iraṇṭu stāyikkuḷ 5vatu taṭavaiyil 300 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷōṭum ṣaṭjamat tiṟku 5:4 pōla varukiṟatu. atiṉuṭaiya cattam ṣaṭjamat tōṭu mikavum cērntu irukkiṟatu. vīṇaiyiṉ 4vatu tantiyil itu rompat teḷivākak kēṭkiṟatu. camaskiruta vitvāṉkaḷ ataṟkup patil itai eṭuttuk koṇṭiruk kiṟārkaḷ. atāvatu 81:64kkup patilāka 5:4 eṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ itait taṅkaḷ ottu varum muṟaiyōṭu (Harmonic) ottirukkiṟateṉṟu collu kiṟārkaḷ.
ni
13. kāntārattai nām ātāra ṣaṭjamamāka vaittuk koṇṭōmāṉāl niṣātam atiṉuṭaiya 5vatu curamākiṟatu. ākaiyiṉāl 300 x 3/2 = 450 = niṣātattiṉuṭaiya ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ.
ataṉ nīḷam 2/3 x 284/5 = 2/3 x 144/5 = 288/15 = 193/15 = 191/5
kuṟippu :- makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl avarkaḷ tuvāvimcati curutiyai aṟivataṟkāka vekunāḷ mikavum ciramappaṭṭirukkiṟāreṉṟu terikiṟatu. intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavai yeṉṟu caṅkītattaiyē toḻilākak koṇṭa vittuvāṉkaḷē vicārikkā tirukkaiyil, tām vicārittu aṟintu, aṟintavaṟṟait tam tēcattār aṟiyap pustaka mūlamākap piracurappaṭuttiyataṟkāka nāṉ mikavum cantōṣappaṭukiṟēṉ. taṟkālattil vaḻaṅki varum caṅkīta cāstiraṅkaḷil irākavipōtamum ataṉpiṉ caṅkīta pārijātamum paḻamaiyāṉa nūlkaḷtāṉ. āṉāl tuvāvimcati curutiyaip paṟṟic collum muṟaippaṭi ivar ceyyāmal irāka vipōta muṟaippaṭic ceytirukkiṟār. pārijātakkārariṉ ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟaippaṭi ivar curaṅkaḷaip paṭippaṭiyāyk kaṇṭupiṭittuk koṇṭu pōvatil 5-vatu paṭiyil kaviliruntu ni kaṇṭupiṭikkum iṭattil kavukku 3033/4 vaipa rēṣaṉil 300 āka eṭuttuk koḷḷac collukiṟār. itaṟku niyāya mirukkiṟateṉṟum collukiṟār. maṟṟavar kātukku iṉimaiyāyirukkiṟa teṉṟatait tavira vēṟu niyāyam etuvum collavillai.
ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟaippaṭi 4vatu aṭukkil atāvatu (1) ca pa (2) pa ri (3) ri ta (4) ta ka vil 33/4 vaiparēṣaṉ pētappaṭumāṉāl maṟṟa ovvoru aṭukkilum koñcam koñcamākak kūṭiyē vantirukka vēṇṭum. itu tavira maṟṟavarkaḷ 4/5 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟa Major Third kku ottirukka vēṇṭumē yeṉṟu niṉaittu 3033/4 il 33/4aik kuṟaittu 240kkum 300kkum eppaṭiyō appaṭi 4:5 irukkiṟateṉṟu collukiṟār. atu tavira 3033/4 ai kāntārat tiṟku mēluḷḷa oru curutiyākac collukiṟār. 240kku 3033/4 eppaṭiyō appaṭi 64kku 81 irukkiṟateṉṟu kaṇakkuk kuṟikkiṟār. 64kku 81 eppaṭiyō eṉṟa kaṇakkai vaittu mēlē pōvataṟkuk kaṭiṉamākum eṉṟu kuṟaittuk koṇṭārō allatu mēṟṟicai caṅkītattil kāṇappaṭum Major Third kkuc cariyāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kuṟaittuk koṇṭārō teriyavillai. āṉāl camaskiruta vittuvāṉkaḷ kātukku iṉimaiyāyirukkiṟateṉṟu colluvatākak kāraṇam collukiṟār. appaṭiyē niṣātam 4555/8 vara vēṇṭiyatāka aḷantu kāṭṭiṉavar 450 āka varavēṇṭumeṉṟum tiruttik koṇṭār. 450kkup piṟaku 4555/8 oru curutiyāka vara vēṇṭumeṉṟu collukiṟār. ippaṭi oru curuti 4vatu 5vatu paṭikaḷil pētappaṭṭu varumpaṭi yāṉa kaṇakkuc cariyāṉa kaṇakkāka irukkumōveṉṟu cantēkikkiṟēṉ. maṟṟum nuṭpamāṉa kaṇakkai itaṉ piṉ varum aṭṭavaṇaiyil viparamāyk kāṇalām.

3-vatu aṭṭavaṇai.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavai yeṉṟu makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi mu.kṣa.tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattaik kāṭṭum tuvāvimcati curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇai.  caṅkīta ratṉākaram, irākavipōtam, pārijātam muṟaippaṭi
* ivvaṭaiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavai
3-vatu aṭṭavaṇaiyil 11-vatu kalattil 8-vatu variyil 300 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ varukiṟatākavum ataṟku aṭutta 9-vatu variyil 303.75 varukiṟa tākavum nām kāṇpōm. ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟaiyāl 4-vatu aṭukkil namakkuk kiṭaikka vēṇṭiyatu 303.75 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷai yuṭaiya kāntāramām. atil 33/4 kuṟaittu 300 āka eṭuttuk koṇṭār. appaṭi eṭuttuk koṇṭālum atu mēṟṟicaiyār muṟaikkuc cariyā yirukkiṟateṉṟu coṉṉāl paravāyillai. cāraṅkar muṟaippaṭi 22 curutikku itu muṟṟilum poruttamuṭaiyatalla. itupōlavē 303.75 iruntu ṣaṭjama pañcamamāyp pōkumpōtu 455.625 vara vēṇṭiyatu. itaṟkup patil cumār aintaraiyēyaraikkāl ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷait taḷḷi 450 eṉṟu 20-vatu variyil pōṭṭirukkiṟār. ituvum mēṟṟicaiyār 8/15 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum niṣātam. immuṟaiyil 8/15 eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum mēṟṟicaiyāriṉ muṟaiyum ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟaiyeṉṟu vaḻaṅkum pārijātakkārar muṟaiyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. ivviru muṟaiyilum kiṭaikkakkūṭiya curaṅkaḷ 22 eṉṟum itu cāraṅkar muṟaiyeṉṟum colluvatu poruntumō? ippaṭippaṭṭa kaṇakku cāraṅkar collavē yillai. intiya caṅkītattukku itu poruttamākavumillai. ivar koṭuttirukkiṟa aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉpaṭi 20 ceṉṭsil 1 curutiyum, 22 ceṉṭsil 6 curutikaḷum, 27 ceṉṭsil 3 curutikaḷum, 63 ceṉṭsil 1 curutiyum, 71 ceṉṭsil 4 curutikaḷum, 84 ceṉṭsil 2 curutikaḷum 90 ceṉṭsil 3 curutikaḷum, 92 ceṉṭsil 2 curutikaḷumāka 22 curutikaḷeṉṟu collukiṟār. cāraṅkatēvarō oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāy uyarntum oṉṟiṟkum maṟṟoṉṟukku muḷḷa iṭaiveḷiyil vēṟu curam varāmalum curuti ceyyac collukiṟār.
ṣaṭjakirāmam, cāntārakirāmam, mattimakirāmameṉṟu cāraṅkar collum muṟaippaṭi kirakacuram pāṭuvataṟku oṟṟumaiyāṉa aḷavuṭaiya vallaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. eṭṭāvatu kalattil ātāra ṣaṭjam 0 āṉāl 1, 2, 3, 4 riṣapaṅkaḷ muṟaiyē 84, 112, 182, 204 eṉṟa ceṉṭskaḷāka varukiṉṟaṉa. itil muṟaiyē 84, 27, 71, 22 eṉṟa ceṉṭskaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. ivai oḻuṅkīṉamāṉa aḷavuṭaiyavaiyeṉṟum kiraka curam pāṭuvataṟku ēṟṟa aḷavillai eṉṟum teḷivākak kāṇpōm. mēlum ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 3, kāntāram 2, mattimam 4, pañcamam 4, taivatam 3, niṣātam 2 eṉṟu ovvoru curattiṟkum ittaṉai curutikaḷ vara vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli mottam 22 eṉṟu coṉṉārē yoḻiya ri, ka, ta, ni eṉṟa curaṅkaḷukku navvālu curutiyirukkiṟatākac collavillai. ākaiyiṉāl itu cāraṅkaruṭaiya karuttallaveṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. maṟṟum ovvoru curutiyaip paṟṟiyum iṅku colla avaciyamillai. aṭṭavaṇaiyil teḷivākak kaṇṭu koḷḷalām.
tantiyaip paṅku vaittuk koṇṭu pōvatu atāvatu 1/2, 1/3, 1/4, 1/5, 1/6 eṉṟu vaittuk koṇṭu pōvatu cariyāṉa aḷavil curaṅkaḷait tarātu. itu curutiñāṉam aṟṟavarkaḷukkē caṟṟu ēṟattāḻac cērttuk koḷḷum vitiyāka ēṟpaṭṭatu. itai Just Temperament eṉṟu colvatu tavaṟutalā yirukkalāmeṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ. 4-vatu paṭiyil 33/4 pētamirukku māṉāl ovvoru paṭiyilum koñcam koñcam pētamirukkum eṉpatu niccayam. niccayamāṉa allatu nuṭpamāṉa oru vaḻi kaṇṭupiṭittiruntā rāṉāl mikavum naṉmaiyāyirukkum. maṟṟum curutikaḷaiyum curaṅkaḷaiyum ivar kuṟikkum muṟaiyāṉatu caṅkīta ratṉākarattiṉpaṭi cariyāṉa teṉṟu nāṉ niṉaikkavillai. ākaiyāl, ataippaṟṟi nāṉ atikamākac colla villai. muṭivāka ivar caṅkīta ratṉākarattil colliya 22 curutikaḷaic collāmal pārijātakkārar curaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭikkum oru aḷavaic colli capta curaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟittuviṭṭu ataṟkup piṉvarum curaṅkaḷai yum curutikaḷaiyum tam maṉampōṉa pōkkil kuṟikkiṟār eṉṟu tōṉṟu kiṟatu. ataṟku enta nūlilum ātāramillai. mēlum kāntārattiṟkum niṣātattiṟkum mēlvarum ciṟu curutikaḷiṉ iṭaiveḷikaḷ pōṉṟa atāvatu 300- 3033/4, 450-4555/8 pōṉṟa ettaṉaiyō iṭaiveḷikaḷ varalām.
itu tavira capta curaṅkaḷiṉ varicaikkiramattil iṭaiveḷikaḷil arai curaṅkaḷiṉ varicaikaḷaiyum, curuti varicaikaḷaiyum kuṟippataṟkāka ivar vaḻaṅki varum muṟai camaskiruta nūlkaḷil collappaṭavillai. tāmāka vaittuk koṇṭatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. ṣaṭjamattiṟkum riṣapattiṟkum naṭuviluḷḷa iṭaiveḷiyai atāvatu 36 aṅkulamutal 32 aṅkula muḷḷa iṭaiveḷiyai 4 curutistāṉaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟār. atil mutalāvatu 1 pōlavum, (2) 21l 20 pōlavum (3) 16l 15 pōlavum (4) 10l 9 pōlavum (5) 9l 8 pōlavum muṟaiyē 36 aṅkulattilum, 342/7 aṅkulat tilum, 333/4 aṅkulattilum, 322/5 aṅkulattilum, 32 aṅkulattilum pākikkiṟār. ivaikaḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu ēṟṟattāṭi nacciyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivai muṟaiyē ātāra ṣaṭjam 240iliruntu 252, 256, 2662/3, 270 āka varukiṉṟaṉa. mutal curuti ātāra ṣaṭjattukku 12 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷ kūṭiyatākavum, iraṇṭāvatu muntiṉataṟku 4 vaiparēṣaṉ kūṭiṉatākavum, mūṉṟāvatu iraṇṭāvataṟku 102/3 kūṭiṉatākavum, nāṉkāvatu mūṉṟāvataṟku 31/3 kūṭiṉatākavum varukiṟatu. ippaṭiyē cila curastāṉaṅkaḷukku oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu ottirāta pala piṉṉaṅkaḷai upayōkikkiṟār. itu ivaruṭaiya conta apippirāyam eṉṟu niṉaikkiṟēṉ. 22 curutikaḷait teḷivāyk kāṭṭa vanta ivar eṅkēyāvatu oru iṭattil tavaṟippōṉāl, tiruttic colvatu pirayōcaṉamāyirukkum. āyiṉum ivar curutikaḷai aṟiya virumpiya viruppattai nāṉ mikavum meccikkoḷḷukiṟēṉ.
ivar ēṟpaṭuttiyirukkum curuti stāṉaṅkaḷaip paṟṟik kiḷameṇṭs eḻutiya apippirāyam aṭiyil varumāṟu :-
In his Introduction to Hindu Musical scale and the 22 Srutis of Deval Mr. E. Clements says
“Secondly it will be found that although Mr. Deval did not test his Shrutees throughout by the harmonic intervals 5:4, 6:5 and 7:6 which may be called the Major Third, Minor Third and Septimal Third, they are clearly built up from those intervals.
“Indeed the importance attached to the Septimal intervals, that is those derived from the seventh harmonic, places the Music of India in the first rank of intellectual developments of the musical art. Some writers on Harmony have elaborated theories based upon the supposition that the subdominant (corresponding with Komal madhyam) and dominant seventh (that is Atikomal madhyam) are for all practical purposes the same note. No one who has attentively listened to Indian Ragas could entertain such an idea for a moment, as the Septimal Seventh, is very much flatter than the ordinary seventh, the interval 7:6 being easily distinguishable even by the untrained ear from the interval 6:5.
“The 22 shrutees are as Mr. Deval points out a selection from the total number of shrutees used in Indian Ragas and Raginis. Mr. Deval had not only to ascertain what shrutees were made use of by different singers but also to pick out the 22 which might justly be considered essential.”
“Mr. Krishnaji Mahadev Gokhale of Miraj who makes use of considerably more than 22 Shrutees has kindly sung over to me 83 Ragas and Raginis and given me the names of the notes used in them. It appeared to me that the extra shrutees used were chiefly those necessary to give the exact septimal intervals in some of those cases which I have marked ‘nearly’ in the footnote. An additional ‘Atikomal Gandhar’ of 280 relative number of vibrations was also used but in one Raga only. Mr. Deval tells me that some singers use a similar septimal ‘Atikomal Nishad’. The ‘Atikomal Nishad’ chosen by him is however a vital necessity as the fourth above madhyam and if the Shrutees are to be restricted to 22 I am afraid one must give up these two septimal notes, pleasing though their effect must be acknowledged to be.
“The intervals between each shrutee and next as given in Mr. Deval’s table D. are of no great significance.”
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutikaḷaip paṟṟik kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
“iraṇṭāvatāka, tēvāl eṉpavar tammuṭaiya curutikaḷ ārmāṉik iṭaiveḷikaḷākiya 5:4, 6:5, 7:6 muṟaiyē atāvatu Major Third, Minor Third and Septimal Third eṉpavaikaḷukku ottirukkiṟatō eṉṟu cōtittup pārkkāviṭṭālum, anta iṭaiveḷikaḷai ātāramāy vaittukkoṇṭu tāṉ curuti niccayam ceytirukkiṟār eṉpataṟkut taṭaiyillai.
 “ēḻāvatu ārmāṉik curamākiya inta Septimal iṭaiveḷiyai vicēṣittuk koḷvatē intiya caṅkītam. yuktipūrvamāy muṉṉukku vantirukkiṟa vittaikaḷil mutaṉmaiyāṉatu eṉpataṟku ōr aṟikuṟiyāyirukkiṟatu. kōmaḷamattimattiṟkuc cariyāṉa Subdominant  (atāvatu ārmōṉiyattil 6vatu curam) um atikōmaḷa mattimattiṟkuccariyāṉa ēḻāvatu Dominant um anēkamāy orē curantāṉ eṉṟa niccayattiṉ pēril cilar pala koḷkaikaḷai stāpittirukkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl intiya irākaṅkaḷai nuṭpamāyk kavaṉitta evarāvatu atu cariyeṉṟu oppukkoḷḷa māṭṭārkaḷ. ēṉeṉṟāl cātāraṇa ēḻāvatu curattaiviṭa Septimal  curamāṉatu curuti kuṟaintatu eṉṟum 7:6 eṉṉum iṭaiveḷiyāṉatu 6:5 eṉṉum iṭaiveḷiyaiviṭa curutiyil kuṟaintatu eṉṟum caṅkīta ñāṉamillātavarkaḷ kūṭa eḷitil kaṇṭukoḷḷalām.
“makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl collukiṟapaṭi 22 curutikaḷum intiya irākaṅkaḷ irākiṉikaḷ ivaikaḷil upayōkikkap paṭukiṟa curutikaḷiṉiṉṟu porunti eṭukkappaṭṭavai. avar pala vittuvāṉkaḷuṭaiya caṅkītaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum iṉṉiṉṉa curutikaḷ avaikaḷil upayōkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu mutalil niccayittup piṉpu vicēṣamāṉavai eṉṟu koḷḷattakka inta 22 curutikaḷai yum avaikaḷiṉiṉṟu poruntiyeṭukka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. 
“22 curutikaḷukku mēl anēka curutikaḷuṇṭu eṉṟu koṇṭa Miraj paṭṭaṇattu Krishnai Mahadev Gokhale eṉpavar 83 irākaṅkaḷ irākiṉikaḷai eṉakkup pāṭikkāṭṭiṉatumallāmal avai kaḷil upayōkikkappaṭum curaṅkaḷiṉ pērkaḷum iṉṉiṉṉavai eṉṟu eṉakkuc coṉṉār. avar coṉṉa 22kku mēṟpaṭṭa curutikaḷ yāvum cariyāy Septimal iṭai veḷikaḷil varakkūṭiya curaṅkaḷ tāṉ. avaikaḷil cilavaṟṟaip pakkattiṉ aṭiyil “nearly”  (atāvatu koñcam kuṟaiya cariyāṉatu) eṉṟa kuṟippāl kāṇpittirukkiṟēṉ. 280 tuṭikaḷuḷḷa iṉṉoru atikōmaḷa kāntāramum orē rākattil upayōkikkappaṭṭatākat terintatu. cila pāṭakarkaḷ atēvitamāy Septimal atikōmaḷa niṣātattai upayōkik kiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu collukiṟār. mattimattiṟku mēl nālāvatu curutiyāka varum inta atikōmaḷa niṣātam kaṭṭāyam irukka vēṇṭiyatu. atillāviṭṭāl jīvaṉillai. āṉāl curutikaḷ irupattiraṇṭu eṉṟu collum viṣayattil mikavum iṉimaiyāṉa inta iraṇṭu cepṭimal curaṅkaḷaiyum viṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭumē eṉṟu atika varuttappaṭukiṟēṉ. 
“makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl pustakattil D. Table eṉṉum aṭṭa vaṇaiyil inta curutikkum aṭutta curutikkum naṭuviluḷḷa iṭaiveḷikaḷaip paṟṟic colliyiruppatāṉatu atika mukkiya māṉatalla.”
Regarding Mr. Deval’s Shrutees Dr. Coomarasamy says :-
“It is true that Mr. Deval did not succeed in his endeavour to improve his case by importing aid and corroboration from scientific acoustics and Sanskrit philogy, but I think that certain of his crities fall into more serious error when they judge the results of his patient and invaluable experimental work by weakness or inaccuracies in his method of presentaiton.”
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutikaḷaip paṟṟi 
Dr. kumāracāmi avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
 “Mr. tēvāl eṉpavar tammuṭaiya koḷkaikaḷai stāpippa taṟkāka nātattaip paṟṟiya kōṭpāṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya cāstira vitikaḷaiyum samskiruta nūlkaḷaiyum ātāramāka eṭutturaittatu avarukku virōtamāy muṭintatēyoḻiya cātakamāy muṭiyavillai. āṉāl avaruṭaiya koḷkai yaip paṟṟit taṅkaḷ apippirāyattai veḷiyiṭṭa anēkar, avar poṟumaiyōṭum viṭāmuyaṟciyōṭum ceytu muṭitta arumaiyāṉa nūlāṉatu avar koḷkaikaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟa viṣayattil kāṇappaṭukiṟa kuṟṟaṅ kuṟaikaḷ tappitaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl viyarttamāy viṭṭatu eṉṟu colvatāṉatu mikavum tappāṉa apippirāyam.”
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tēvāl intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum tuvāvimcati curutikaḷaic colla vantavar aṅkaṅku cila culōkaṅkaḷai eṭuttu mēṟkōḷākac collikkoṇṭu mēṟṟicai caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ collum eṉ ārmāṉik skēl (Enharmonic Scale) eṉṉum 18 curaṅkaḷi liruntu 13 curaṅkaḷai appaṭiyē eṭuttuk koṇṭu ataṟku mēlum kīḻumākac cila curutikaḷaik kūṭṭi 22kku niraval ceytirukkiṟār. mēṟ kāṭṭiya 13 curaṅkaḷ varuvataṟkāka caṅkīta pārijātakkārar muṟaiyaic coṉṉār. maṟṟum 9 curutikaḷ varuvataṟkut takutiyāṉa niyāyam collavillai. intiyāvilē piṟantu vaḷarntu intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷaic colla vantavar taṟkālattil vaḻaṅki varum kāṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa irākam iṉṉa iṉṉa curutikaḷil varukiṟateṉṟu vistāramāy vicārittu aṉupava muṟaippaṭic curutikaḷaic collavu millai; allatu caṅkīta ratṉākarar curuti cērkkum muṟaippaṭic curuti cērttu atai stāpikkavu millai; allatu caṅkīta pārijātakkārar colliya vaḻippaṭip pōy intiya caṅkīta curutikaḷ ippaṭittāṉ eṉṟu oru uṟuti kūṟavumillai. intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷ vāyiṉāl pāṭavum atu pōla vīṇaiyil vācittuk kāṭṭavum kūṭiyatē yoḻiya curutikaḷuk kēṟṟa meṭṭu vaittu vīṇaiyil vācippatum ārmōṉiyam mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷil curam amaippatum avaikaḷil pāṭuvatum muṟṟilum kūṭāta kāriyam. appaṭic ceyyap pirayattaṉappaṭuvatu intiya caṅkītattiṉ vistārattaik keṭuttu ataṉ aḻakum uṉṉatamumāṉa cikaraṅkaḷaiyum uṭaittup pāṭina paṭuttuvatākum. mēlum curutikaḷai aḷantu curam kaṇṭu piṭikkum pārijātakkāraruṭaiya muṟaiyaip pōlotta orumuṟai yaiyē mēṟṟicaiyārum aṉucarittiruppatākat terikiṟatu. eṉṟālum avarkaḷ curaṅkaḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu oṟṟumaippaṭṭu iṉimai taruvataṟku aṉukūlamāka mēṟkaṇṭa aḷaviṉāl kiṭaitta curaṅkaḷukkukkūṭṭiyum kuṟaittum taṟkālam vaḻaṅkik koṇṭu varukiṟārkaḷ. eṉ ārmāṉik skēl (Enharmonic Scale) eṉṟu eṭuttuk koṇṭavar avaikaḷil koñca māvatu māṟṟāmal eṭuttuk koṇṭālum oruvāṟu cariyāyirukkum. āṉāl ‘caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭaiya apippirāyam itutāṉ, intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ ivaikaḷ tāṉ’ eṉṟu coṉṉatu caṟṟuk kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. eṉṟālum, intiya caṅkītattil taṉakkut terinta apippirāyaṅkaḷaip piracurittu neṭunāḷ kavaṉikkāmal viṭṭuviṭṭa intiyarukku oru kiḷarcci uṇṭāvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyirunta ivaruṭaiya muyaṟci mikavum pārāṭṭat takuntatāyirukkiṟatu.









nālāvatu.
ṭisṭirikṭ jaṭj E. kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ curutiyiṉ muṟai.
kiḷameṇṭs (Mr. Clements) avarkaḷ intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷaip paṟṟi iraṇṭu vitamāṉa kaṇakkukaḷaik koṭukkiṟār. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu pātar, cāraṅka tēvarkaḷuṭaiya apippirāyappaṭi 22 curuti kaḷaip paṟṟiyatu. maṟṟoṉṟu K.B. tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattaik kalantu tām cila cērttuc colluvatu. ivviraṇṭu apippirāyaṅ kaḷilum cila mukkiyamāṉa vacaṉaṅkaḷaiyum kaṇakkukaḷaiyum iṅkē eṭuttuc colli ataṉpiṉ cila kuṟippukaḷaic colvatu mikavum avaciyamā yirukkiṟatu.
Introduction to the study of Indian Music, By E. Clements I.C.S. P.2.
“The present work deals with Hindustani music only; the author hopes to be able to show that a great part of it is directly traccable to the systems set forth in Bharatha’s Natya-Shastra of about the fifth century A.D., and the Sangit Ratnakar of the thirteenth century. These are the most closely reasoned and critically worded of the early text books. It is reputed that Sarangadev the author of the Sangit Ratnakar was an inhabitant of Kashmir. From internal evidence one would conclude that the music he describes is that if Hindustan. However the pandits of Southern India endeavour to appropriate him to themselves. The present writer hopes to show that it it only by doing violence to his theory that it can be applied to Karnatic music. Roughly speaking, Hindustani music may be said to prevail in the north and west of India and the eccan, while Karnatic music is confined to the south and cast. Many scales are common to both but the general spirit of the two systems is apparent from the scales which are first taught to beginners; in the west, the scale is the same as the just major scale of Europe in the south it is a chromatic scale (known in Hindustani music as the scale of the Raga Bhairava) with semitones between the first and second, third and fourth, fifth and sixth, seventh and eighth degrees.”
 “innūl intustāṉi caṅkītattaippaṟṟi māttiram collap pōkiṟatu. itil collappōkiṟa anēka caṅkatikaḷ, ki.pi. aintāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa parataruṭaiya nāṭṭiya cāstiram eṉṉum nūlil collappaṭṭa muṟaikaḷi liruntum, ki.pi. patiṉmūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil uṇṭāṉa caṅkīta ratṉākaram eṉṉum nūliliruntum nērē eḻutap paṭṭavai eṉṟu innūlāciriyar tiruṣṭāntappaṭuttuvār. ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷuntāṉ āti caṅkīta nūlkaḷil tarkka cāstira muṟaikaḷuk kiṇaṅka eḻutappaṭṭavai. caṅkīta ratṉākara nūlāciriyarāṉa cāraṅka tēvar eṉpavar kāsmīra tēcattaic cērntavar eṉṟu oppuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. uḷ nuḻaintu anta nūlai vācittāl avar pēcum caṅkītam intustāṉi caṅkītameṉṟu naṉṟāyt teriyum. āṉālum teṉṉintiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ avarait taṅkaḷuk kuriyavareṉṟu koṇṭāṭukiṟārkaḷ. tammuṭaiya koḷkai intustāṉi caṅkītattukkuriyavaiyēyallāmal karnāṭaka caṅkīttukkuriyavaiyalla eṉṟu innūlāciriyar tiṭṭa māy rūpakāram paṇṇappōkiṟār. mēmpāṭāyc colla vēṇṭu māṉāl intustāṉi caṅkītamāṉatu intiyāviṉ vaṭakku, mēṟku, ṭakkāṉ (Deccan) pākaṅkaḷilum, karnāṭaka caṅkītam teṟkilum, kiḻakkilum upayōkikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu eṉṟu collalām. iraṇṭu caṅkītattukkum anēka mūrccaṉaikaḷ potuvāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl piratamattil ciṣyar kaḷukkuc collik koṭukkappaṭum mūrccaṉaikaḷaik kavaṉittāl iraṇṭiṉuṭaiya amcaṅkaḷukkum uḷḷa vittiyācam teriyum. mēṟku tēcattil collik koṭukkappaṭum mūrccaṉai airōppiya mējar skēl (Major Scale) ai ottirukkiṟatu. teṟkē collik koṭukkappaṭuvatu arai curaṅkaḷākiya kurōmāṭik skēl (Chromatic Scale). itil muṟaiyē mutalāvatu iraṇṭāvatu curattiṟkum, mūṉṟāvatu nāṉkāvatu curattiṟkum, aintāvatu āṟāvatu curattiṟkum ēḻāvatu eṭṭāvatu curattiṟkum naṭuvē arai curaṅkaḷ varum. itai intustāṉi caṅkītattil pairavi rākam eṉpārkaḷ.”
mēlē kaṇṭa cila vacaṉaṅkaḷiṉāl tāṉ paratar cāraṅkatēvar kaḷuṭaiya muṟaiyai aṉucarittē 22 curutikaḷaic collappōkiṟa tākavum ammuṟaiyum intustāṉi caṅkītattiṟkē uriyateṉṟum teḷivākac collukiṟār. inta iṭattil cāraṅkatēvar haitarāpāt (Hyderabad) eṉṉum intustāṉi tēcattil oḷaraṅkapāt eṉṉum periya paṭṭaṇattukku arukiluḷḷa taulatapāt eṉṉum intustāṉi paṭṭaṇattil iruntataṉāl intustāṉikkuriya caṅkīta muṟaikaḷai eḻutiyirukkiṟār eṉṟu kiḷameṇṭs niṉaikka ētuvirukkiṟatu. eṉṟālum uṇmaiyil avar nūlai vicārippōmāṉāl anēka amcaṅkaḷil appaṭiyallaveṉṟu teriyavarum.
mutalāvatu, tuvāvimcati curutiyaip paṟṟi eṭuttuk koḷvōm. tēvālum kiḷameṇṭsum collum apippirāyattiṟkuc caṅkīta ratṉākarariṉ apippirāyam muṟṟilum vēṟāyirukkiṟatu. kiḷameṇṭs collukiṟa apippirāyam intustāṉ caṅkītattiṟkēyuriyatu eṉṟu tiṭṭamāyc collukiṟapaṭi avviṭattil intustāṉi caṅkītattil pēr peṟṟa vittuvāṉ aptul karīm (Abdul Karim) mūlamāyt tām muka mukamāyk kēṭṭu oppuk koḷḷukiṟa curutikaḷāṉavai intustāṉi caṅkītattukkē yuriyatāyirukkalām. āṉāl cāraṅkatēvaruṭaiya tuvā vimcati curuti muṟaikku ottatāyirukka māṭṭāteṉṟu itaṉ piṉ varum cāraṅkatēvar tuvāvimcati curuti aṭṭavaṇaiyil vivaramāyk kāṇalām.
iraṇṭāvatu, cāraṅkatēvar intutēcavāciyākiya cimmaṇarājaṉ camastāṉa vittuvāṉāyiruntatākavum avaruṭaiya kēṭṭuk koḷḷutaliṉ pēril caṅkītaratṉākaram eḻutiṉatākavum terikiṟatu.
mūṉṟāvatu pirammā, viṣṇu, uruttiraṉ mutaliya mummūrtti kaḷaiyum akāra, ukāra makāra, ōṅkāram eṉṉum pījāṭcāṅkaḷaiyum, nālu vētaṅkaḷaiyum, nālu jātikaḷaiyum 7 curaṅkaḷukkum ati tēvataikaḷāṉa akkiṉi, pirammā, carasvati, araṉ, ari, vināyakaṉ, cūriyaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ pērkaḷaiyum, avaikaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭitta intiraṉ, pirammā, cantiraṉ, viṣṇu, nāratar, tumpuru eṉṉum periyōrkaḷiṉ pērkaḷaiyum, irākaṅkaḷiṉ pērkaḷaiyum kavaṉikkaiyil intustāṉi caṅkītattiṟkum caṅkīta ratṉā karattukkum evvitamāṉa campantamumillai yeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
nālāvatu makammatu kajiṉi paṭaiyeṭuttu vanta kālattaiyum (1024), ṭilliyil 1206l mutal mutal rājyam stāpitta makammatu kōriyiṉ kālattaiyum, tēvakiriyil cōmarāja makārājaṉ kālattaiyum nām ottiṭṭup pārttāl, cāraṅkatēvar intustāṉi caṅkītattiṟkāka inta nūl eḻutiyirukkamāṭṭāreṉpatu veḷiyākum. vintiya malaikkut teṉpākattiṟku makamatiyar 1294-m varuṣattil vantatākat terikiṟatē yoḻiya ataṟku muṉ vantatākat teriyavillai. eppaṭiyiruntālum intustāṉ caṅkītattiṟkuriya curutikaḷai eḻutukiṟēṉ eṉṟu colvatil namakku evvitamāṉa āṣēpaṉaiyumillai. āṉāl cāraṅka tēvar muṟaippaṭi itai eḻutukiṟēṉ eṉṟu colluvatutāṉ muṟṟilum āṣēpaṉaikku iṭaimāyirukkiṟatu. kiḷameṇṭs tammuṭaiya pustakam 6-vatu 7-vatu pakkaṅkaḷil piṉ varumāṟu collukiṟār.
Introduction to the study of Indian Music by E. Clements. P. 6, 7.
“He persevered for years at this investigaiton deriving assistane from many of the best singers that India could produce. As regards most of the notes in use, his conclusions, when referred back to ancient theory, may be summed up in the statement that two Srutis make a just semitone, three Srutis a minor-tone, and four Srutis a major-tone. In respect of these notes the accuracy of his conclusions can fairly be said to be beyond controversy.
*    *    *    *    *    *    *    *    *    *    *    *
“The author has through Mr. Deval’s courtesy, and with the help of Abdul Karim and other singers, been able to verify all the varidus scales mentioned in the following pages upon this instrument (Deval’s Sruti Harmonium).
“The following table describes the twenty-four notes in most frequent use, showing which of them are adopted in the Indian Harmonium, and their relationship with the ancient Srutis.”
“intiyāvil pūrṇa vittuvāṉkaḷ eṉṟeṇṇappaṭṭa mutal taramāṉa pāṭṭukkārariṉ utaviyaik koṇṭu inta curuti ārāycciyil avar anēka varuṣaṅkaḷaic celavaḻittār. ātiyil ēṟpaṭṭa vitikaḷiṉ muṟaippaṭip pārttāl, upayōkattilirukkappaṭṭa curaṅkaḷaip paṟṟi avar colva tāṉatu tokai yāyp piṉ varumāṟākum. atāvatu, iraṇṭu curutikaḷ koṇṭatu oru jasṭ semiṭōṉ (Just semitone) mūṉṟu curutikaḷ koṇṭatu oru maiṉar ṭōṉ (minor tone)  nāṉku curutikaḷ koṇṭatu oru mējar ṭōṉ (major tone)  eṉpatē. anta curaṅ kaḷaip paṟṟi avar muṭivāyc colluva tāṉatu anēkamāyp piḻaiyillāmal cariyākavēyirukkiṟatu eṉṟu ellārum oppukkoḷvārkaḷ. 
“piṉvarum pakkaṅkaḷil collappaṭṭa pala ārōkaṇaṅkaḷai yum inta vāttiyattil (curuti ārmōṉiyam) tēvāl eṉpavaruṭaiya tayaviṉālum aptul karīm mutaliya pāṭṭu vittuvāṉkaḷiṉ utaviyaik koṇṭum cōtittu niccayit tirukkiṟārkaḷ. aṭiyil varum aṭṭavaṇaiyāṉatu atikamāy upayōkattilirukkappaṭṭa 24 curutikaḷ evaiyeṉṟum avai kaḷil evaikaḷ intiya ārmōṉiyattil varukiṟateṉṟum, avaikaḷukkum paḻaiya curutikaḷukkum irukkum tāratam miyam eṉṉaveṉṟum kāṭṭukiṟatu.”
mēlē kaṇṭa vākkiyaṅkaḷil tām intustāṉi caṅkīta vittuvāṉ aptul karīmaik koṇṭum tēvāliṉ ārāycciyil kaṇṭupiṭitta curaṅkaḷaik koṇṭum curutikaḷaip paṟṟip piṉ varum aṭṭavaṇai tayār ceytatākac collukiṟār.














4-vatu aṭṭavaṇai.
intiyāviluḷḷa intustāṉi caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavai yeṉṟu
E.kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattaik kāṭṭum
curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇai.
* ivvaṭaiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavai
			
		kuṟippu : inta aṭṭavaṇai muṟṟilum tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ pustakattil 29-vatu pakkattiluḷḷa D aṭṭavaṇaiyākavēyirukkiṟatu. eṉṟālum tēvāl eṭuttuk koṇṭa 10-vatu curutikkup piṉ oru curutiyum, 17-vatu curutikkup piṉ oru curutiyum, nūtaṉamāyc colluvatē itil kavaṉikkappaṭa vēṇṭiyatu. pattāvatu curutikku 320 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷum 11-vatu curutikku 3371/2 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷu mirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu tēvāl collukiṟār. atai kiḷameṇṭs oppuk koṇṭu 320 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷukkuppiṉ 324 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya oru curutiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu collukiṟār. appaṭiyē tēvāl colliya 17-vatu curuti 405 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷai yuṭaiyateṉṟum 18-vatu curuti 4262/3 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya teṉṟum oppukkoṇṭu, itaṉ naṭumattiyil 405kkup piṟaku 420 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya oru curutiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum collukiṟār. ivviraṇṭu curutikaḷum aptul karīmāl pāṭappaṭṭu mikunta iṉimai yuṭaiyatākat tam aṉupavattil kaṇṭa curaṅkaḷ eṉṟum collukiṟār. ivviraṇṭu nuṭpamāṉa curaṅkaḷaiyum tam kātiṉāl kēṭṭu aṟintu koṇṭatum tāṉ kēṭṭatai evvita cantēkamumiṉṟi maṟṟavarkaḷukkuc colla niṉaittatum mikavum mēlāṉatē. oru mēṟṟicai caṅkīta vittuvāṉ intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷai ārāyvatum, avaikaḷ iṉṉatu tāṉ eṉṟu niccayippatum ilakuvāṉa kāriyamalla. ivviraṇṭu curutikaḷ mattimattiṟku mēl oṉṟum, taivatattiṟku mēloṉṟumāka iruppatu pōla maṟṟa curaṅkaḷukku naṭumattiyilum ivaṟṟaip pōlavum ivaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟitākavum anēkam vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu nam intiya tēcattuc caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷil evarum kaṇitappaṭi rucuppaṭuttik kāṭṭak kūṭiyavarkaḷ alla. āṉāl paṭittukkāṭṭa māttiram kūṭiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. itaṉālēyē intiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷukkuḷ curuti viṣayamāy oṟṟumai yuṇṭākavillai. 320kkum 3371/2kkum, 405kkum 4262/3kkum uḷḷa iṭaiveḷikaḷaip pōlavē, 4555/8kkum 480kkum naṭuvilum, 432kkum 450kkum, 384kkum 400kkum naṭuvilum, 360kkum 378kkum naṭuvilum, 3441/3kkum 360kkum naṭuvilum, 288kkum 300kkum naṭuvilum 270kkum 2844/9kkum naṭuvilum, 240kkum 252kkum naṭuvilum pōla iṉṉum ettaṉaiyō iṭaṅkaḷil nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷ varalām. 320kkup piṉ 324l oru curuti pēcukiṟateṉṟāl, 328l oṉṟum 332l oṉṟum ēṉ pēcamāṭṭātu? appaṭiyē 4262/3l oru curutiyum 420l oru curutiyum pēcumāṉāl, 414l oṉṟum 408l oṉṟum ēṉ pēcakkūṭātu? ippaṭi yiruppataṉāl mikunta oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyatum iṉimaiyuṭaiyatumāṉa anēka curutikaḷ intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu nām aṟiya vēṇṭum. avaikaḷaiyellām piṉvarum teṉ intiya caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷaip paṟṟic collum aṭṭavaṇaiyil kāṇalām.
mēṟṟicaiyār curaṅkaḷukkuc collum ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷai ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu tēvāl ceyta tiruttaṅkaḷukku upayōkitta kaṇakkukaḷ cariyallaveṉṟu coṉṉatu pōlavē itaṟkum colla vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu.
21-vatu curutikkut tēvāl 4555/8 eṉṟu collukiṟār. āṉāl kiḷameṇṭs 4551/9 eṉṟu collukiṟār. itu tāṅkaḷ eṭuttukkoṇṭa piṉṉaṅkaḷiṉāl vanta coṟpak kuṟaivu. itu oṉṟu tavira, maṟṟa enta eṇkaḷilum tēvālai ottirukkiṟār.











aintāvatu.
caṅkīta ratṉākarar muṟaippaṭi kirāmam māṟṟukaiyil nūtaṉamākak kiṭaikkiṟateṉṟu
E.kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷ collum 3 curutikaḷ. 
caṅkīta ratṉākaram 3-vatu pirakaraṇam 24 mutaṟ koṇṭu 38 varaikkumuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷiṉpaṭi ceyyappaṭṭatu.
inta aṭṭavaṇaiyil 3 curutikaḷai 22 curutikaḷōṭu cērttuc colli yirukkiṟār. 22 curutikaḷai ippaṭic ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟu coṉṉa caṅkīta ratṉākararē ṣaṭjama kirāmam, mattima kirāmam, kāntāra kirāmam eṉṉum mūṉṟu kirāmattil curutikaḷai māṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu colliyirukkiṟatiliruntu inta nūtaṉamāṉa 3 curutikaḷum kiṭaittaṉa eṉṟu collukiṟār. atāvatu 300 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya antara kāntārattukkuk kīḻ 2968/12 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya oru curutiyum 360 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya pañcamattiṉ kīḻ 3555/9 ōcaiyiṉ alai kaḷaiyuṭaiya oru curutiyum varukiṟateṉṟum 450 ōcaiyiṉ alai kaḷaiyuṭaiya kākali niṣātattiṟkuk kīḻ 4441/9 ōcaiyiṉ alai kaḷaiyuṭaiya oru curutiyum varavēṇṭumeṉṟum collukiṟār. tēvāl avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattil, mutal curuti 252 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷukkup patil 250 eṉṟum oṉpatāvatil 315 ōcaiyiṉ alaikaḷukkup patil 3161/8 eṉṟum, 12vatil 3411/3 kkup patil 3453/5 eṉṟum, 14vatil 378kkup patil 375 eṉṟum nālu iṭaṅkaḷil coṟpa vittiyācam collukiṟārēyoḻiya maṟṟa ellā viṭaṅkaḷilum, avaruṭaiya apippirāyattait taḻuviyē eḻuti yirukkiṟār. caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭaiya apippirāyattiṉpaṭi ṣaṭjama mattima kāntāra kirāmaṅkaḷ vaittukkoṇṭu pōkuṅ kālattil 22 curutikaḷait tavira vēṟu enta curutikaḷum piṟakkamāṭṭā. arai arai curaṅkaḷāy amainta oru stāyiyil oru curam māṟṟik koṇṭu mēl pōvataṉāl atil irukkum curaṅkaḷtāṉ piṟakkumēyallāmal naṭuvil vēṟu oru curam piṟakkamāṭṭātu. atupōlavē 22 curutikaḷuḷḷa stāyikaḷil ṣaṭjama mattima kāntāra kirāmaṅkaḷ māṟṟuvataṉāl 22 curutiyiṉ varicaiyil varumēyoḻiya vēṟu varicai uṇṭākamāṭṭātu. kirāmam eṉpatu ovvoru curamum ittaṉai ittaṉai curutiyōṭu varavēṇṭumeṉṟu amaittuk koṇṭu ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇa vitippaṭi kāṉam ceyvatām. kirāmattiṟkāka curutikaḷai eppaṭi māṟṟik koṇṭālum, mottattil ellā curutikaḷum cērntu 22 tāṉirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu potuvāy amaintirukkiṟatu. ivaṟṟil ṣaṭjama kirāmamāvatu ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 3, kāntāram 2, mattimam 4, pañcamam 4, taivatam 3, niṣātam 2 eṉṟu 22 curutikaḷ varuvatām. ivai muṟaiyē ṣaṭjamattiṉ 4, 3, 2, 1 eṉṟa curutikaḷil ārampittu muṭivaṭaiyum. mattima kirāmam ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 3, kāntāram 2, mattimam 4, pañcamam 3, taivatam 4, niṣātam 2 eṉṉum 22 curutikaḷōṭu varuvatu. ituvum muṉpōlavē ṣaṭjamattiṉ 4, 3, 2, 1 eṉṉum curutikaḷiṉ muṟaiyē ārampittu sapta cura kiramappaṭi kāṉam ceyyappaṭum. kāntāra kirāmamāvatu ṣaṭjamam 4, riṣapam 2, kāntāram 4, mattimam 3, pañcamam 3, taivatam 3, niṣātam 3 āka 22 curutikaḷaiyumuṭaiya sapta curaṅkaḷ muṟaiyē ṣaṭjamattiṉ 4, 3, 2, 1 eṉṉum curutikaḷil ārampittuk kāṉam ceyyappaṭuvateṉṟē colla vēṇṭum.













5-vatu aṭṭavaṇai.
intustāṉi caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉavaiyeṉṟu
E..kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattaik kāṭṭum curutiyiṉ aṭṭavaṇai.
caṅkīta ratṉākara muṟaippaṭi ṣaṭja, mattima, kāntāra kirāmaṅkaḷai māṟṟumpōtu, kūṭutalākak kiṭaikkum mūṉṟu curutikaḷ.
* ivvaṭaiyāḷamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷ innūlukkuriyavai
kiraka māṟṟum viṣayattaippaṟṟi mēṟcolliya cila vacaṉaṅkaḷai aṭiyil varum aṭṭavaṇai mikat teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
* ṣaṭjakirāmam.
caṅkīta ratṉākarar colliya muṟaippaṭi

* mattimakirāmam.
caṅkīta ratṉākarar colliya muṟaippaṭi

* kāntārakirāmam
caṅkīta ratṉākarar colliya muṟaippaṭi

tiruṣṭāntamāka ṣaṭja kirāmattil ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālu curutikaḷai yum ovvoṉṟāka iṭatupakkam taḷḷikkoṇṭu pōkaiyil mēl varicaiyiṉ ṣaṭjamattiṉ iraṇṭām curutiyil ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālu curuti vaittu ārampippōmāṉāl iraṇṭa curutikaḷaiyuṭaiya kāntāramum niṣātamum riṣapa taivataṅkaḷil layam aṭaikiṉṟaṉa. atāvatu riṣapa taivatattiṉ mūṉṟām curutiyē kāntāramākavum niṣātamākavum ākiṟatu. ippaṭiyē ṣaṭjamattiṉ mutal curutiyil ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālām curutiyai vaittu ārampikkumpoḻutu mūṉṟu curutikaḷuṭaiya riṣapamum taivatamum ṣaṭjama pañcamaṅkaḷil layam aṭaikiṉṟaṉa. atāvatu ṣaṭjamam riṣapamākavum, pañcamam taivatamākavum varukiṟatu. irupattiraṇṭām curutiyākiya niṣātattil nālām curuti ārampikkum pōtu niṣātattil ṣaṭjamamum kāntārat til mattimamum mattimattil pañcamum pēcukiṟatu. appōtu taivatattiṉ 18, 19, 20 eṉṟa mūṉṟu curutikaḷil iraṇṭāvatākiya pattoṉpatāvatu curutiyil mutalāvatu ṣaṭjam ārampikkiṟatu. itupōlavē ovvoru kirāmamum taṉ taṉ curattiṉ eṇkaḷōṭum aḷavōṭum kiraman tavaṟāmal naṭaipeṟum. Mr. kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷiṉ curutik kaṇakkiṉ aḷavaiyum kaṇakkaiyum itaṉpiṉ aṭṭavaṇaiyākak koṭuttirukkiṟōm. anta aḷavai arai aṅkula akalamuḷḷa nīḷamāṉa nālu tuṇṭu kaṭitaṅkaḷil kuṟittuk koṇṭu oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyk kirakam māṟṟi vaittup pārppā mēyāṉāl avaikaḷ ērāḷamāṉa iṭaiveḷikaḷaiyum curaṅkaḷaiyu muṇṭākkumeṉpatai pirattiyaṭcamāy aṟiyalām.
mēlum itaṉ muṉṉuḷḷa aintāvatu aṭṭavaṇai oṉpatāvatu kalattil curutikaḷiṉ iṭaiveḷikaḷai ceṉṭs kaṇakkil colli yiruppataik kavaṉittāl 22, 41, 49, 71, 90 ceṉṭskaḷ eṉṟu aintu pētattil collukiṟār. āṉāl nālāvatu aṭṭavaṇaiyil 20, 22, 27, 63, 71, 84, 90, 92 ceṉṭskaḷāṉa eṭṭupētamāṉa aḷavukaḷ colliyirukkiṟār. ivvaḷavu pētamāṉa aḷavukaḷ, cāraṅkar muṟaippaṭi curuti māṟṟum poḻutu, evvitattilum ottuvarāta ērāḷamāṉa curutikaḷait tarum eṉṟu aṟivāḷikaḷ kavaṉippārkaḷ. mūṉṟu curaṅkaḷ kirakamāṟṟumpōtu uṇṭākiṟateṉṟu coṉṉatu poruntātatāyiruntālum pāṭakarkaḷaik koṇṭu maṟṟavarkaḷ 22 eṉṟu collum curaṅkaḷukkumēl 3 curaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittu niccayittuk kuṟittatāṉatu nām meccik koḷḷak kūṭiyatu.
caṅkīta ratṉākarattil kaṇṭa immūṉṟu kirāmaṅkaḷait tavira vēṟu kirāmamum paḻamaiyāṉa tamiḻ nūlākiya cilappatikārattil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. atil marutayāḻ, kuṟiñciyāḻ, neytalyāḻ, pālaiyāḻ eṉṟa 4 kirāmaṅkaḷ iruntatākat terikiṟatu. ivaikaḷil marutayāḻ kural allatu ṣaṭjamam, 4 tuttam allatu riṣapam 4, kaikkiḷai allatu kāntāram 3, uḻai allatu mattimam 2, iḷi allatu pañcamam 4, viḷari allatu taivatam 3, tāram allatu niṣātam 2 āka 22 curutikaḷum 22 alakākac collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaik kavaṉikkaiyil ṣaṭja mattima kāntāra kirāmameṉṟu caṅkīta ratṉākarar collum mūṉṟu vitamāṉa muṟaikaḷukkum itu vēṟāṉa muṟai eṉpatākat terikiṟatu. itait tavira, āyappālai, tirikōṇappālai, caturappālaiyeṉṉum mūṉṟu muṟaikaḷukkum alaku collappaṭavillai. ivaikaḷai naṉṟāyk kavaṉikkaiyil oru stāyiyil 22 curutikaḷai ēḻucuraṅkaḷukkum pākittu, iṉṉa iṉṉa aḷavil ēḻu curaṅkaḷ varavēṇṭu meṉṟu coṉṉatēyoḻiya vēṟillai. inta 22 curutikaḷuḷ kirakam māṟṟuvataṉāl ṣaṭjamattiṉ curutikaḷ nālum, muṟaiyē ovvoṉṟāy iṭam pērntu vaḻaṅkukiṟa muṟai oṉṟum ṣaṭjamattiṉ nālāvatu curuti yiliruntu valam pērntupōkiṟa muṟaiyoṉṟumāka iraṇṭu muṟai iruntatākat terikiṟatu. ivaikaḷil valam pērntu varum muṟai caṅkīta ratṉākarattil kāṇappaṭavillai. āṉāl mikappaḻamaiyāṉa tamiḻ nūl kaḷil collappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṉum maṟṟa vivaraṅkaḷ yāvaiyum piṉṉāl varum aṭṭavaṇaiyil kaṇṭu koḷka.
Mr. kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷ caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭaiya tuvāvimcati curutikaḷiṉpaṭi intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ eṉṟu colliyirukkum aṭṭavaṇaiyil 15 curaṅkaḷ iṅkilīṣ eṉ ārmāṉik skēli (Enharmonic Scale) liruntu eṭukkappaṭṭavai. maṟṟum 10 curaṅkaḷil 3 curutikaḷ kirāmam māṟuvatiṉāl uṇṭāṉavaiyeṉṟum mītiyāṉa 7 curam 25 curutiyaic cērntateṉṟum collukiṟār.
mottamāka Mr kiḷameṇṭs eḻutiya pustakattaip pārkkaiyil tēvāl avarkaḷuṭaiya curuti muṟaiyiṉāl vaḻi tavaṟip pōyirukkalām eṉpatēyoḻiya maṟṟappaṭi avaruṭaiya nuṭpamāṉa vicāraṇaikkāka nāṉ mikavum cantōṣappaṭukiṟēṉ. aptul karīm mutaliya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ pāṭit tām kēṭṭatil ivaikaḷ intustāṉi rākattiṟku varukiṟateṉṟu colluvatai nāṉ oppuk koḷkiṟēṉ. vaḻakkattil muṟṟilum ēṟukku māṟāṉa anēka intustāṉi muṟaikaḷaippōl ituvum intiya caṅkītattiṟku vittiyācamākayirukka vēṇṭum. āṉāl mottamāy oru stāyikkuḷ curutikaḷai eṅkē eṭuttukkoṇṭu kāṉam paṇṇiṉālum avaikaḷukku oru oḻuṅkāṉamuṟai irukka vēṇṭiyatu ciramameṉṟu nāṉ niṉaikkiṟēṉ. caṅkīta ratṉākarar camaskirutattil eḻutiyavaṟṟai vācikkāmalum, vācittālum arttam teriyāmalum, vārttaiyiṉ aḷavāka arttam terintālum uḷporuḷ aṟiyāmalum, uḷporuḷ terintum cātaṉaikkuk koṇṭu varāmalum, ēmāntuniṟkum intiya vittuva cirōmaṇikaḷ ettaṉaiyō pērkaḷirukka, Mr. kiḷameṇṭs avarkaḷ tuvāvimcati curutikaḷaippaṟṟi vicārikkavum vicārittavai kaḷil muṭivāṉa vaikaḷaip piṟarukkut terivikkavum muṉ vantatu mikavum mēṉmai yāṉatē.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷukkut takunta vitamāy ārmōṉiyam ceyvatu kūṭātakāriyamāyiruntālum, cila curutikaḷai amaittu ārmōṉiyattil colluvatāṉatu, itupōl iṉṉum anēka curutikaḷ varalāmeṉṟu niccayippataṟku oru mutaṟpaṭi pōlirukkiṟatu eṉṟu pukaṭinantu collak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu.
maṟṟum ivaruṭaiya curutimuṟaiyil kavaṉikkavēṇṭiya mukkiya kuṟippukaḷai nām iṅku collāmal viṭṭālum aṭṭavaṇaiyil teḷivākak kāṇalām. 



āṟāvatu.
III. cāraṅkar curuti muṟaippaṭi teṉṉintiya caṅkītam irukkiṟateṉṟu collum
mūṉṟām vakuppār.
Retired Inspector of Schools  makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi rāv pakatūr
C. nākōji rāv avarkaḷiṉ 22 curutiyiṉ muṟai.
intiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum 22 curutikaḷaip paṟṟi makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi nākōjirāv avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam iṅkilīṣil eḻutap paṭṭut tañcāvūr Training School 1st Assistant  makā-rācarāca- ciṟi cuntaramaiyar B.A.L.T. avarkaḷāl tamiḻil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭu, tañcai caṅkīta vittiyā makājaṉa caṅkattiṉ 2-vatu kāṉperaṉsil paṭikkappaṭṭatu.
iṅku avviyācattiṉ cila mukkiya pākaṅkaḷaip pārppōm :-
caṅkīta vittiyā makājaṉa caṅkam 2-vatu kāṉpareṉs rippōrṭ pakkam 53-66.
“namatu caṅkītattil oru stāyiyil 12 curastāṉaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu caṅkītavittuvāṉkaḷ collukiṟārkaḷ, intap paṉṉiraṇṭum, (sa),cutta riṣapam (ri), catur curuti riṣapam (ri), allatu cutta kāntāram (ka), sātāraṇa kāntāram (ka), allatu ṣaṭcuruti riṣapam (ri), antarakāntāram (ka), cutta mattiyamam (ma), piratimattiyamam (ma), pa, cutta taivatam (ta), catur curuti taivatam (ta), allatu cuttaniṣātam (ni), kaicika niṣātam (ni), allatu ṣaṭ curuti taivatam (ta) kākali niṣātam (ni) ākiya ippaṉṉiraṇṭumām. itaiyē airōppiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ aintu muḻuc curaṅkaḷum ēḻu araic curaṅkaḷum ākap paṉṉiraṇṭu curastāṉaṅkaḷ eṉṟu collu kiṟārkaḷ. 
mēlē coṉṉa curapētaṅkaḷait tavira, iṉṉum ciṟu pētaṅ kaḷuḷḷa curaṅkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum avai namatu caṅkītattil varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum collukiṟārkaḷ. cilar, airōppiyaratu caṅkītattil illāta 1/4 curaṅkaḷum iṉṉum ciṟiya curaṅkaḷum varukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu eḻutukiṟārkaḷ. mēṟcoṉṉa 12 curaṅkaḷil, orē vakai ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇa curaṅkaḷuḷḷavaiyāyiruntu pāṭiṉāl kātil vēṟu vēṟu rākaṅkaḷākap pulappaṭum rākaṅ kaḷaip paṟṟik kēṭṭāl, namatu caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ, antanta rākaṅkaḷil kamaka pētaṅkaḷiruppatāl vēṟuvēṟu rākaṅkaḷāy viṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu vivaramillāta oru maṟumoḻi colli viṭukiṟārkaḷ. namatu cāstira pustakaṅkaḷil, oru stāyiyil iruppatu 22 curaṅkaḷ allatu curutikaḷ eṉṟu viyaktamāy eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. intak kāraṇaṅkaḷai yellām koṇṭu nām muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭiyateṉṉa veṉṟāl, namatu caṅkītattil iruppatu 12 curaṅkaḷ alla, ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭuttāṉ irukka vēṇṭumeṉpatē. 
iṉi nām aṟiyavēṇṭiyateṉṉaveṉṟāl oru stāyi yil niccayamāy ettaṉai curaṅkaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum avai eṉṉaveṉṉa eṉpatumām. 
nām ārāyntu pārttamaṭṭil, namatu caṅkītattil oru stāyi yil namatu cāstirap pustakaṅkaḷiṟ colliyatu pōla 22 curaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. mēlum inta 22 curaṅ kaḷait tavira vēṟu curaṅkaḷ varamuṭiyāteṉṟum terikiṟatu. 
airōppiya caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷ oru curam eṉpatu antac curam cattikkum ātāramāṉa vastu etuvō atu naṭuṅkuvatāltāṉ uṇṭākiṟateṉṟu collukiṟārkaḷ. mēlum kuṟippiṭṭa nēra moṉṟil anta naṭuṅkum vastu atikamāṉa taṭavai naṭuṅkiṉāl mēl curamum kuṟainta taṭavai naṭuṅkiṉāl tāṭi nanta curamum uṇṭākum eṉṟum kaṇṭaṟintirukkiṟārkaḷ. mēlum ca eṉpatu evvaḷavu vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl (Vibration- vēpaṉam) allatu ōcaiyalaikaḷāl uṇṭākiṟatō ataiviṭa iraṭṭippu maṭaṅku vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl mēl stāyi ca uṇṭākiṟateṉṟum kaṇṭaṟintiruk kiṟārkaḷ. 
antak kaṇakkuppirakāram pārttatil oru stāyiyil piṉ varukiṟa 22 curaṅkaḷum varum.

inta varicaiyil ovvoru curattiṟkum etiril eḻuti yirukkum kaṇakku antac curam (1)ca eṉpatum ettaṉai vēpaṉaṅ kaḷāl āṉatō ataiviṭa ittaṉai maṭaṅku vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl āṉatu eṉpataik kuṟikkiṟatu. ca eṉpatu 240 vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl āṉāl, ri (1) (mutal riṣapam) 250 vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākiṟatu. ri (2) (iraṇṭāvatu riṣapam) 256 vēpaṉaṅkaḷāl ākiṟatu. atē mātiri kaṇakkiṭṭup pārttuk koḷḷavum. 
ippaṭi 22 curaṅkaḷtāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ataṟkumēl irukka muṭiyātu eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ iṉṉavaitāṉ eṉṟu mutalil eḻutukiṟēṉ. 
airōppiyaratu saṅkītattil, sa, ka(3), pa, sa1, ākiya inta nālu svaraṅkaḷum cērntu captittāl iṉṉavaitāṉ eṉṟu mutalil eḻutukiṟēṉ. 
airōppiyaratu saṅkītattil, sa, ka(3), pa, sa, ākiya inta nālu svaraṅkaḷum cērntu captittāl kātukkuc cukattait tarukiṉṟaṉa. inta nālu svaraṅkaḷukkum “cērum svaraṅkaḷ” eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkukiṟārkaḷ. ippaṭi cērntu svaraṅkaḷ captippatiṉāl  uṇṭākum cukattukku hārmani (harmony) eṉṟu peyar. airōppiyaratu saṅkītam muḻuvatum ippaṭi hārmaniyē niṟaintatām. 
ippaṭi cukam uṇṭāvataṟkuk kāraṇam uṇṭu. ateṉṉa veṉṟāl, oru tanti vāttiyattil sa eṉṉum captam uṇṭākumpaṭi oru tantiyai mīṭṭiṉāl, ataṉuṭaṉē atē kālattil ka(3), pa, sa1, ākiya immūṉṟu svaraṅkaḷum uṇṭāki nam kātil viḻukiṉṟaṉa. (mēlum oru piṭil tantiyil villaip pōṭṭuk koṇṭu iṭatu kai viralāl tantiyait toṭṭatum toṭātatu māka viralai mēlē iḻuttuk koṇṭē pōṉāl sa, ka(3), pa, sa1 inta nālu svaraṅkaḷ maṭṭil nam kātil viḻukiṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya vēṟu svaraṅkaḷ nam kātil viḻukiṟatillai.) eṉavē, namatu kātu sa eṉpatu captikka kēṭṭavuṭaṉē ka(3), pa, sa1 inta svaraṅkaḷaiyum kēṭka cittamākiviṭukiṟatu. anta svaraṅkaḷ antac camayattil kātil viḻuntāl cukattai uṇṭākku kiṉṟaṉa. intac cukattukku hārmani eṉṟu peyar. inta svaraṅkaḷukku svapāvattil cērum svaraṅkaḷ (Chord of nature) kārṭ āp nēccar eṉṟu peyar. atē mātiriyāy, inta svaraṅkaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉṉoṉṟāy anukkiramamāka vantāl anta cukattukku melaṭi (melody) allatu (namatu saṅkītattil uḷḷa) rākam eṉṟa peyar vaḻakkattil irukkiṟatu. appaṭi cukam taruvataṟkuk kāraṇamum, muṉ coṉṉa mātiri inta svaraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērntu vantāl hārmaniyait taruvatutāṉ. 
sa, ka(3), pa, sa1 eṉṉum nālu svaraṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk koṇṭāl sa1 eṉṉum svarattiṟkum sa eṉṉum svarat tiṟkum iṭaiveḷi 2 ākiṟatu. atāvatu sa1 eṉpatu sa-vai viṭa iraṇṭu maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl āṉātu. (inta iraṇṭu svaramum atika oṟṟumaiyuḷḷaṉavātalāl cērntu vantāl nalla hārmaniyait taruvataṉālum, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu naṉṟāy iḻaintu koṇṭu taṉittaṉi svaraṅkaḷākak kātukkup pulappaṭātirukkiṟataṉālum inta iraṇṭu svaraṅ kaḷukkum orē peyar vaittirukkiṟārkaḷ) ākavē nalla iṭaiveḷikaḷil 2-m oṉṟām. 
piṟaku, pa eṉpataṟkum sa eṉpataṟkum iṭaiveḷi 3/2. atāvatu pa, sa-vai viṭa 3/2 allatu 11/2 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl āṉatu. inta iraṇṭu svaraṅkaḷum naṉṟāyc cērakkūṭiya svaraṅkaḷtāṉ. āṉāl sa’-vum sa-vum evvaḷavu naṉṟāyc cērumō avvaḷavu naṉṟāy avaikaḷ cērukiṟatillai. ākaiyiṉāl 3/2 eṉpatu iṉṉoru nalla iṭaiveḷiyākiṟatu. (inta mātiri, sa, pa, sa’ immūṉṟum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu naṉṟāy cērvatu paṟṟi, vīṇai mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷil sa, pa, sa’ eṉṟu karuti vaittuk koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ.) 
itē mātiri kaṇakkiṭṭup pārttāl sa-vukkum pa-vukkum iṭaiveḷi 4/3 ākiṟatu. atu piṉ varumāṟu :-
pa x 4/3 = sa’
3/2 x 4/3 = 2 
ka(3)-vukkum sa-vukkum iṭaiveḷi 5/4 ākiṟatu. pa-vukkum ka(3)-vukkum iṭaiveḷi 6/5 ākiṟatu. atu piṉ varumāṟu : 
ka(3) x 6/5 = pa. 
5/4 x 6/5 = 3/2 
mēlē coṉṉatiliruntu nām muḻutum aṟintu koṇṭa teṉṉaveṉṟāl :- 
2 allatu 2/1, 3/2, 4/3, 5/4, 6/5 
inta iṭaiveḷikaḷ nalla iṭaiveḷikaḷ. inta iṭaiveḷi kaḷuḷḷa svaraṅkaḷ anukkiramamāka vantāl rākam naṉṟāy uṇṭākum eṉpatutāṉ. 
mēlum, itaiviṭa vēṟu cila ciṟiya iṭaiveḷikaḷuḷḷa svaraṅkaḷ cērntālum cukam tarumeṉṟu airōppiya saṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ apippirāyam koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ. avaikaḷāvaṉa:-
(1) sa-vukku 9/8 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākum mējar ṭōṉ (major tone)  allatu periya svaram.
(2) sa-vukku 10/9 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākum mainar ṭōṉ (minor tone) allatu ciṉṉa svaram.
(3) sa-vukku 16/15 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākum mējar semiṭōṉ (major semitone) allatu periya arai svaram.
 (4) sa-vukku 25/24 maṭaṅku vēpanaṅkaḷāl uṇṭākum mainar semiṭōṉ (minor semitone)  allatu ciṉṉa arai svaram ākiya ivaikaḷām.
antak kāraṇattaikkoṇṭē mēlē coṉṉa iṭaiveḷi kaḷuḷḷa svaraṅkaḷ anukkiramamāka vantālum melaṭi (melody)  allatu rākam eṉṉum cukattait tarum eṉṟum nām aṟintu koḷḷalām. ēṉeṉil, cila svaraṅkaḷ cērntāl hārmani uṇṭākum pakṣattil avaikaḷ anukkiramamāy vantāl melaṭi uṇṭākum eṉṟu ūkikkalāmallavā?
itiliruntu nām kāṇpatu eṉṉaveṉṟāl 25/24, 16/15, 10/9, 9/8, 6/5, 5/4 inta iṭaiveḷikaḷuḷḷa svaraṅkaḷ vantāl cukattait tarum eṉpatē.
sa, pa, sa1 immūṉṟu svaraṅkaḷum cērntu vantāl hārmani irukkiṟateṉṟu vitvāṉkaḷ yārum oppuk koḷḷukiṟapaṭi yāl avaikaḷuḷḷa iṭaiveḷikaḷum nalla iṭaiveḷi kaḷākum. avai 4/5, 3/2, 2 āka im mūṉṟumām.
1. nām iṉi kavaṉikkavēṇṭiyateṉṉaveṉṟāl, sa eṉpataṟ kum ka(3) eṉpataṟkum iṭaiyil cērumpaṭiyāṉa iṭaiveḷi kaḷuḷḷa enta enta svaraṅkaḷ varalām eṉṟu pārkka vēṇṭiyatutāṉ. atē mātiri ka(3) vukkum, pa vukkum, pa vukkum sa1 vukkum iṭaiyil enta svaraṅkaḷ varalām eṉṟu pārkka vēṇṭum.
mutalil sa eṉpataṟkum ka(3) vukkum iṭaiyil ri (1) eṉṉum svaram vantāl naṉṟāyc cērum. ēṉeṉṟāl ri (1) kkum sa vukkum iṭaiveḷi 25/24 ka(3) vukkum ri (1) kkum iṭaiveḷi 6/5, kāraṇam:-
25/24 x 6/5 = 5/4
ri (1) x 6/5 = ka(3).
atāvatu ri(1) yaic curutiyāka vaittuk koṇṭāl ka(3) eṉpatu anta curutikku sātāraṇa kāntāramākap pēcum. piṟaku, ri (3) eṉṉum svaramum sa vukkum ka(3) vukkum iṭaiyil vantāl iraṇṭuṭaṉum naṉṟāyc cērum. ēṉeṉṟāl (ri3) kkum sa vukkum iṭaiveḷi 10/9 ka(3) vukkum ri(3) kkum iṭaiveḷi 9/8. itaṟkuk kāraṇam :-
10/9 x 9/8 = 5/4
ri(3) x 9/8 = ka(3).
atāvatu ri(3) yaic curutiyāy vaittuk koṇṭāl, ka(3) ataṟku ri(4) ākap pēcum.
piṟaku ri(4), sa vukkum, ka(3) vukkum iṭaiyil vantāl cukattait tarum. ēṉeṉṟāl ri (4)kkum sa vukkum iṭaiveḷi 9/8. ka(3) vukkum ri(4)kkum iṭaiveḷi 10/9. itaṟkuk kāraṇam :-
9/8 x 10/9 = 5/4
ri(4) x 10/9 = ka(3)
atāvatu ri(4) eṉpataic curutiyāy vaittuk koṇṭāl ataṟku ka(3), ri(3) āyp pēcum.
6/5, 9/8, 10/9 inta iṭaiveḷikaḷ mēlē eḻutiyirukkum iṭaiveḷikaḷuḷ oṉṟākum eṉkiṟataiyum kāṇka.
